# How to Use This Manual

This manual explains how to use AGRINUXES from the first sign-up to the last report. It covers every kind of account, every menu and tab, every form, and the template system in detail. It was written from the platform itself as it stood in October 2026. Where something is not finished, only works on one device, or shows sample data, the manual says so plainly.

You do not have to read it from front to back. Pick your starting point below.

## Where to start

| If you are… | Read these parts |
|---|---|
| A **farmer or cooperative** | Part 1 (account and set-up), then Part 2 (templates), Part 3 (production), Part 4 (business), Part 5 (management and tools) |
| An **agro-dealer**, **AgriSupply** provider or **AgriFood** seller | Part 1, Part 6 (workspace basics, invoices, revenue), Part 7 (your tabs) |
| An **agro processor** | Part 1, Part 6 (workspace basics), Part 7 (processing, sourcing and traceability) |
| A **vet**, **equipment-hire**, **transport** or **AgriServices** business | Part 1, Part 6 |
| An **individual buyer** | Part 1, Part 8 (chapter on individual accounts) |
| An **extension officer or supervisor** | Part 1, Part 8 (chapter on extension officers) |
| **Government** staff | Part 1, Part 8 (chapter on government accounts) |
| A **programme partner** (NGO, donor, programme manager, M&E officer) | Part 1, Part 8 (chapter on programme partners) |
| The **owner or administrator** of a company | Part 1, especially Team and Permissions, Data-sharing consent and Backup and Restore |
| Mainly interested in **templates** | Part 2, then Appendices A to D |

## How the manual is organised

| Part | What it covers |
|---|---|
| Part 1 | Getting started: account types, registering, signing in, the set-up wizard, finding your way around, settings, team and permissions, data sharing, backup, and where your data is kept |
| Part 2 | Templates, record fields and care guides: choosing, using, customising and saving templates; the daily log; the care plan; spreadsheet import |
| Part 3 | Running production: cycles, stages, events, outputs and closing a cycle |
| Part 4 | Running the farm business: dashboard, processing, inventory, sales, trade and export, finance, credit profile and reports |
| Part 5 | Farm management, intelligence and devices: animal health, staff, procurement, crop rotation, AI pages, device pages and the marketplaces seen from a farm |
| Part 6 | Vendor workspaces: the parts every vendor shares, then vets, equipment hire, transport and AgriServices |
| Part 7 | Vendor workspaces: agro-dealers, AgriSupply, AgriFood sellers and processors |
| Part 8 | Buyers, government, extension officers and programme partners |
| Appendices | A: template catalogue. B: daily record fields. C: cycle types. D: starter care guides. E: roles and permissions. F: current limits and preview features. G: glossary. H: troubleshooting and questions. I: first-week checklists |

## How to read the pages

- Names of buttons, tabs, menu items and fields are written in **bold**, exactly as they appear on screen.
- Instructions you follow are numbered steps. Do them in order.
- Boxes call attention to something:

> **Tip:** A shortcut or good habit that saves time.

> **Watch out:** Something that can cause a mistake, lost work or a wrong figure.

> **Note:** Extra information worth knowing.

> **Example:** A worked example with realistic numbers.

- Amounts are shown in **ZMW** (Zambian kwacha) where the platform shows ZMW. Your company's currency is chosen when you set up.

## Three things to know before you begin

1. **Some information is shared by your whole company and some is not.** Your farm workspace (business profile, enterprises, cycles, daily records, events, saved templates), Finance, Sales and Inventory are kept in your company account, so everyone in the company sees the same thing on any device. Several other pages are kept only in the browser on the device you used. Part 1 has a full table, and every page in Parts 4 and 5 says which kind it is.
2. **What you see depends on your role.** The owner can do everything. Other team members see only the menu items their role allows (Appendix E lists every role).
3. **Starter care guides are a starting point, not medical or agronomic advice.** Always confirm vaccines, medicines, doses and sprays with a vet, an agronomist or an extension officer, and read the product label, before you rely on them (Part 2).

# Part 1 — Getting Started and Your Account

## What AGRINUXES is and who it is for

AGRINUXES is an online platform for agriculture in Zambia and the region. It does two jobs at once. First, it is a **marketplace**: a place where farmers, buyers, vets, equipment owners, input shops and transporters find each other. Second, it is a **business workspace**: a set of tools that a farm or an agribusiness uses every day to record production, stock, sales and money, and to manage staff.

You may see the name written as **AgroNexus** on some screens (the sign-in box, the top of the sidebar, the individual-customer page) and as **AGRINUXES** on others (the set-up wizard and the vendor sidebar). It is the same product.

Everything in this manual is read from how the platform actually behaves today. Where a feature is not yet finished, or is kept only in your browser, the manual says so plainly.

### The account types

When you register you choose one of three routes: **Individual**, **Business** (which includes farms), or **Gov / Partner**. Behind those three routes there are several kinds of account. The table shows what each one is for and what you get.

| Account type | Who it is for | What you get |
|---|---|---|
| Individual (customer) | Buyers and anyone who only wants to browse and contact providers | A simple customer page: Home, Marketplace, Saved, Profile. No business workspace. |
| Farmer / Farm | A farm of any size: crops, poultry, livestock, fish, mixed | The full **farm workspace**: production, inventory, sales, finance, reports, AI tools, devices, team |
| Cooperative | A smallholder group running shared crops, livestock and marketing | The same full farm workspace as a farm |
| Agro Dealer | Input shops: seeds, fertiliser, chemicals, feed | A **vendor workspace** with catalogue, orders, stock, clients, invoices |
| Vet / Animal Health | Veterinary practices | Vendor workspace with appointments, patient records, prescriptions |
| Equipment Hire | Owners of tractors, implements, drones, irrigation kits | Vendor workspace with fleet, bookings, maintenance |
| AgriFood Seller | Sellers of fresh produce and food products | Vendor workspace with produce listings, buyer orders, pricing and stock |
| AgriSupply Provider | Suppliers of packaging, materials, chemicals, storage | Vendor workspace with supply catalogue, purchase orders, deliveries |
| AgriServices Provider | Irrigation, fencing, soil testing, consulting, land preparation | Vendor workspace with services catalogue and job schedule |
| Agro Processor | Millers, oil pressers, dairy and meat processors | Vendor workspace with farm sourcing, processing logs, traceability |
| Transport / Haulage | Hauliers moving produce and inputs | Vendor workspace with transport jobs, vehicles, maintenance |
| Government | Ministry and district-office staff | A read-only dashboard of de-identified figures from farms that opted in, plus advisories and programmes |
| Extension officer / supervisor | Government field officers | A caseload of farms in your camp or district, visits and follow-ups |
| Partner | NGO, donor and development-programme staff | Programme tools (enrolment, support, results) and the same district data |

Farmers and cooperatives get the **farm workspace**. Every other business type gets a **vendor workspace** with its own tabs. Parts 2 to 5 explain the farm workspace. Parts 6 and 7 explain the vendor workspaces. Part 8 explains buyers, government, extension officers and partners.

> **Note:** The label you pick at registration is not always the type you end up with. For example, "Crop Farm" and "Poultry Farm" both become a plain **Farm**. The full list is in the chapter "Registering for an account".

## Visiting without an account

You do not need an account to look around. When you open AGRINUXES and are not signed in, you land on the public **marketplace page**.

### What a visitor can do

**Before you start:** nothing. Just open the site.

**What you will see**

- A top bar with the AGRINUXES name and a **Marketplace** badge, plus **Sign in** and **Register free** buttons. On a phone the two buttons sit behind a menu icon at the top right.
- A large banner that says **Browse free — no account needed**, with two buttons: **Register as Individual** and **Register Farm / Business**. Both open the same sign-in and registration window.
- Four small boxes that summarise the offer: the number of listings, "Verified sellers", "AI farm tools" (for business accounts) and "Instant booking" (once registered).
- A **search box** ("Search providers, products, categories…") and a row of category buttons.
- A grid of cards.

**The categories**

| Category | What is listed |
|---|---|
| Vet Services | Mobile vets, clinics, aquaculture vets, remote vet consultations, para-vets |
| Equipment Hire | Tractors, irrigation kits, drone spraying, refrigerated trucks |
| Agro Dealers | Input suppliers, feed mills, solar pumps, chemical distributors |
| AgriFood | Broilers, tilapia, tomatoes, maize, milk, honey |
| AgriSupply | Seed, fertiliser, feed, sprayers |
| AgriServices | Crop advisory, soil and water labs, farmer training, farm finance and insurance |

Each category button shows how many listings it holds. **All** shows everything.

**What a card shows:** a photo, the category, a **Verified** badge when the seller is marked verified, the name, a one-line description, the price (for products, in ZMW per unit), the town, the star rating and number of ratings (for providers), how many units are available and who sells them, and a "From ZMW …" price for services.

**Opening a card:** click it. A window shows the full description, the tags (such as "VBZ Registered" or "24/7"), the location and the price. It has these buttons:

- **Call directly** — starts a phone call to the provider. This is free and needs no account.
- **Video call** — appears only on listings tagged for video or teleconsultation. It asks you to sign in.
- **Order** (for products) or **Book / Enquire** (for providers) — asks you to sign in.

A yellow note at the bottom reminds you: sign in or register free to book, order and start video calls.

### What needs an account

Booking, ordering and video calls. A visitor can browse, search, filter and phone any provider, but nothing else.

> **Note:** The cards on this public page are sample listings that are built into the page. They show you how the marketplace looks and what a listing contains. They are not a live list of real sellers. The live marketplace for your own business is reached after you register (see Parts 6 and 7).

### Good to know

- Search looks at the name, the one-line description, the category and the tags. If nothing matches you see **No results for "…"** and a **Clear filters** link.
- The number of listings under the search box updates as you filter. The note "phone calls always free" is shown next to it.
- A footer link, **Sign in / Register**, opens the same window.
- If you are already signed in and somehow land on this page, it shows "Signed in as …" and a **Go to dashboard** button.

## Registering for an account

All registration happens in one window that opens when you press **Sign in**, **Register free**, **Register as Individual** or **Register Farm / Business**. The window has four tabs along the top: **Sign In**, **Individual**, **Business** and **Gov / Partner**. Press the tab for the route you want. Switching tabs clears what you typed.

Things that are the same on every route:

- **Phone number is always required.** The registration will not go through without it.
- **Password rules:** at least 6 characters, and you must type it twice. The small eye icon in the box shows or hides what you typed.
- **Terms:** you must tick **I agree to the Terms of Service and Privacy Policy**. The create button stays grey until you tick it (on the Gov / Partner tab you get a message instead).
- **Email** must be a real email-shaped address (it must contain an @). Each email can be used for one account only. Each phone number can also be used for one account only.
- Pressing the grey **X** at the top right, or clicking outside the window, closes it.

### Route 1: Individual

**What it is for:** buyers, farmers and customers who want to browse and contact providers without running a farm system. You can upgrade later by registering a separate Business account (use a different email and phone number).

**Step by step**

1. On the landing page press **Register free** (or **Register as Individual**).
2. Press the **Individual** tab if it is not already open.
3. Type your **Full name**.
4. Type your **Email address**.
5. Type your **Phone number** (the example shown is +260 977 000 111).
6. Optionally pick a **Country** (Zambia, Zimbabwe, Malawi, Tanzania, Mozambique, Botswana, Kenya, Uganda or Other).
7. Type a **Password** (min 6 characters) and type it again under **Confirm password**.
8. Tick the Terms box.
9. Press **Create Account**.

**What you will see:** you go straight to your customer page (see "The individual customer page" later in this part).

| Field | Required? | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Full name | Yes | |
| Email address | Yes | Must be unused |
| Phone number | Yes | Must be unused |
| Country | No | Shown as "Country (optional)". It is not stored with the account. |
| Password | Yes | At least 6 characters |
| Confirm password | Yes | Must match |
| Terms tick-box | Yes | Button stays disabled until ticked |

At the bottom there are shortcuts: **Register a farm or business instead** and **Sign in**.

### Route 2: Business (farms and every vendor type)

Business registration has **two steps**. A bar at the top of the window shows "Step 1 of 2" and "Step 2 of 2".

**Step 1 — your account details**

1. Press the **Business** tab. A green note explains that a Business / Farm account gives you full farm management (production cycles, staff, payroll, inventory, AI analytics, IoT and the complete marketplace).
2. Type **Your full name (director / owner)**.
3. Type your **Email address**.
4. Type your **Phone number**.
5. Type and confirm your **Password** (min 6 characters).
6. Tick the Terms box.
7. Press **Continue to farm details**.

**Step 2 — your farm or business**

1. Type the **Farm / business name**.
2. Open **Type of farm / business** and pick one (full list below).
3. Pick your **Country** from the list (it starts on Zambia; the list is Zambia, Zimbabwe, Malawi, Tanzania, Mozambique, Botswana, Namibia, Angola, Congo DRC, Kenya, Uganda, Other).
4. Optionally fill **Farm size (ha)** and **Staff / users**.
5. Press **Create Farm Account**. Press **Back** if you want to change step 1.

**What happens next:** the account is created, you are signed in, and you are taken to the **set-up wizard** (see "The set-up wizard").

You become the **director** of the new company and its administrator. The director has full access to everything and manages the team.

| Field | Step | Required? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Your full name | 1 | Yes | |
| Email address | 1 | Yes | |
| Phone number | 1 | Yes | |
| Password / Confirm | 1 | Yes | Min 6 characters |
| Terms | 1 | Yes | |
| Farm / business name | 2 | Yes | Becomes the company name |
| Type of farm / business | 2 | Yes | Decides your workspace |
| Country | 2 | Defaults to Zambia | Not stored at this point; you choose again in the wizard |
| Farm size (ha) | 2 | No | Not stored |
| Staff / users | 2 | No | Not stored |

> **Watch out:** **Farm size**, **Staff / users** and **Country** in step 2 are not saved with your account. You will be asked for your country and currency again in the set-up wizard, and your farm size can be entered later where the farm needs it.

#### Every business type: the form label and what you get

| Label in the registration form | You become | Workspace you get |
|---|---|---|
| Crop Farm | Farm | Farm workspace |
| Poultry Farm | Farm | Farm workspace |
| Livestock / Cattle Farm | Farm | Farm workspace |
| Aquaculture / Fish Farm | Farm | Farm workspace |
| Mixed Farm | Farm | Farm workspace |
| Other | Farm | Farm workspace |
| Cooperative / Smallholder Group | Cooperative | Farm workspace |
| Agro Dealer / Input Shop | Agro Dealer | Vendor workspace: Product Catalog, Orders, Stock Management |
| Veterinary / Animal Health | Veterinary Services | Vendor workspace: Appointments, Patient Records, Prescriptions |
| Equipment Hire | Equipment Hire | Vendor workspace: Fleet Management, Bookings, Maintenance |
| AgriFood Seller / Trader | AgriFood Seller | Vendor workspace: Produce Listings, Buyer Orders, Pricing & Stock |
| AgriSupply Provider | AgriSupply Provider | Vendor workspace: Supply Catalog, Purchase Orders, Deliveries |
| AgriServices Provider | AgriServices Provider | Vendor workspace: Services Catalog, Job Schedule |
| Agribusiness / Processing | Agro Processor | Vendor workspace: Farm Sourcing, Processing Logs, Traceability |
| Transport / Haulage | Transport & Logistics | Vendor workspace: Transport Jobs, Vehicles, Maintenance |

> **Tip:** The registration form describes the *kind of farm* (crop, poultry, fish). What you actually produce is chosen later in the wizard, as "enterprises", so the six farm labels all lead to the same place.

### Route 3: Gov / Partner

**What it is for:** ministry and district staff, extension officers, and NGO, donor and development-partner staff. This route gives **read-only, de-identified** dashboards (production, prices, disease hotspots and sustainability figures added up across farms that have opted in) and lets you send advisories by district.

**Step by step**

1. Press the **Gov / Partner** tab. An amber note explains what these accounts see.
2. Type your **Your full name**.
3. Type your **Official email address**.
4. Type your **Phone number**.
5. Type your **Organisation** (for example "Ministry of Agriculture — Chongwe DACO").
6. Pick the **organisation type**: **Government ministry / district office**, **NGO / development partner**, or **Donor programme**.
7. Choose what you will do, by pressing one of three boxes:
   - **Dashboard viewer** — aggregated district and national data
   - **Extension officer** — caseload of farms in your camp or district
   - **Supporting partner (FAO, donor, NGO)** — run programmes: enrol farms, deliver inputs and training, track results. Choosing this box also switches the organisation type from Government to Donor programme if it was on Government.
8. Fill **Province** and **District**. They are optional for viewers and partners, and **required for an extension officer** (the boxes then read "Province" and "District" without "(optional)").
9. Type and confirm your **Password** (min 6 characters).
10. Tick the Terms box.
11. Press **Create partner account**.

**What you will see:** you are signed in and taken to the matching dashboard: the government dashboard (viewer), the extension workspace (officer) or the partner workspace (supporting partner). Those are explained in Part 8.

| Field | Required? | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Your full name | Yes | |
| Official email address | Yes | |
| Phone number | Yes | |
| Organisation | Yes | Becomes the organisation name |
| Organisation type | Yes | Government / NGO / Donor |
| Role box | Yes | Dashboard viewer is the default |
| Province, District | Extension officer only | Optional otherwise |
| Password, Confirm, Terms | Yes | Min 6 characters |

> **Note:** The person who registers an organisation becomes its first administrator. They can then add colleagues with the right roles from **Team & Branches** (see "Team and Permissions").

### If something goes wrong during registration

| Message | What it means and what to do |
|---|---|
| Please agree to the Terms of Service to continue. | Tick the Terms box. |
| Passwords do not match. | The two password boxes differ. Retype both. |
| Password must be at least 6 characters. | Use a longer password. |
| Please enter a phone number. | Phone is mandatory. Fill it in. |
| Please fill in all required fields. | Business step 1: name, email or password is empty. |
| Please enter your farm name and type. | Business step 2: both are required. |
| Phone number is required. | The phone number reached the server empty. |
| A user with this email already exists. / An account with this email already exists. | That email is taken. Sign in instead, or use another email. |
| A user with this phone number already exists. | That phone number is taken. |
| Invalid business type. / Invalid role selected. / Invalid organisation type for a government / partner account. | You should not normally see these. Reload the page and try again. |
| Something went wrong. Please try again. | A temporary problem on the server. Wait a moment and retry. |
| Network error … | Your internet connection or the server is down. Check your connection. |

## Signing in, signing out and your session

### Signing in

**Step by step**

1. Press **Sign in** (top right of the landing page). The **Sign In** tab opens with the heading **Welcome back**.
2. Type your email in the first box (placeholder "Email address").
3. Type your password. Use the eye icon to show it.
4. Press **Sign In**.

**What happens next**

- An **Individual** account goes to the customer page.
- A **Farm** or **Cooperative** account goes to the farm workspace (or to the set-up wizard if the company has not finished set-up yet).
- Any other **Business** type goes to its vendor workspace.
- **Government**, **Extension** and **Partner** accounts go to their own dashboards.

If you have a team member who was added without an email address, the platform stores a made-up internal address for them and they are meant to sign in with their phone number. The sign-in box is labelled "Email address", and some browsers refuse text without an @ in that kind of box.

> **Tip:** Give every team member a real email address when you add them. It is the most reliable way to sign in.

### If sign-in fails

| Message | What it means |
|---|---|
| Invalid phone/email or password. | The email (or phone) or password is wrong. Retype carefully. Passwords are case sensitive. |
| Account is disabled. | An administrator has deactivated your account. Contact them. |
| Please sign in to continue. | Your session has ended. Sign in again. |
| Network error … | No connection to the server. |

### Forgot password

The sign-in window has a **Forgot password?** link. **It does not do anything yet.** There is no self-service way to reset a forgotten password and no screen to change a password inside the app. If you forget your password, contact your company administrator or AGRINUXES support. Keep your password safe, and when an administrator gives you a temporary password, note that you cannot change it from inside the app today.

### Signing out

- **Individual customers:** **Sign out** is in the top bar (and in the menu on a phone), and again at the bottom of the **Profile** page.
- **Vendor workspaces, Government, Extension and Partner:** **Sign Out** is at the bottom of the left sidebar.
- **Farm and Cooperative workspace:** the left sidebar does **not** have a sign-out button. Your session ends by itself after 8 hours (see below). On a shared computer, always use a private browser window, or clear the site's saved data in your browser when you finish.

When you do sign out, the platform also removes the saved copy of the company workspace that it keeps in your browser, so the next person on the same device does not see your company.

> **Watch out:** On a computer that other people use, never leave a farm workspace open. Because there is no sign-out button in the farm sidebar, close the private window or clear the browser's data for the site.

### Sessions and signing in on a second device

- Signing in gives your browser a pass that lasts about **8 hours**. After that, the next time the page loads you are sent back to the public page and must sign in again.
- You can be signed in on **several devices at the same time**. A new sign-in on a phone does not sign out your laptop. Both see the same company data (see "Where your data is kept").
- Closing the browser tab does not sign you out. Opening the site again on the same device in the next 8 hours brings you straight back in.
- A person who is deactivated by an administrator can no longer sign in.

## The set-up wizard

The first time a director of a Farm, Cooperative or vendor company signs in, the platform shows a **set-up wizard** before anything else. It prepares your company workspace. The wizard has five steps, shown as numbered dots along the top: **Welcome**, **Business Type**, **Your Business**, **Setup**, **Ready**. Vendors skip the last step and go straight to their workspace.

**Before you start:** register a Business account first (see "Registering for an account"). The wizard needs about two minutes.

> **Watch out:** Do not add team members until the owner has finished the wizard. A team member who signs in while the company workspace is still empty is shown the wizard too, and could start setting it up in the owner's place.

> **Watch out:** Pick the **same business type** in the wizard that you chose at registration. The wizard does not pre-select your registration choice (it always starts on **Farmer / Farm**). If you pick a different type, your account is updated to the new type, but the screen you are looking at may still follow the old one until you reload or sign in again.

### Step 1: Welcome

A welcome screen titled **Welcome to AGRINUXES** with four small boxes (All business types, Auto-configured, Marketplace access, Grow together). Press **Get started**.

### Step 2: Business Type

The heading is **What type of business are you?** Press one card. A tick shows your choice. The choice decides which workspace you get: farm tools (Farmer or Cooperative) or vendor tools (everything else).

| Card | Description shown | Workspace after set-up |
|---|---|---|
| Farmer / Farm | Manage crops, livestock, fish, and farm cycles | Farm workspace |
| Agro Dealer | Sell seeds, fertilizers, pesticides & farm inputs | Vendor: Agro Dealer |
| Vet / Animal Health | Veterinary services, consultations & treatments | Vendor: Vet Services |
| Equipment Hire | Rent out tractors, harvesters & machinery | Vendor: Equipment Hire |
| AgriFood Seller | Sell fresh produce, food products & farm outputs | Vendor: AgriFood Seller |
| AgriSupply Provider | Supply farm materials, packaging & logistics | Vendor: AgriSupply |
| AgriServices Provider | Irrigation, fencing, soil testing & other services | Vendor: AgriServices |
| Agro Processor | Process raw farm produce into finished goods | Vendor: Agro Processor |
| Transport / Haulage | Move produce and inputs between farms, depots and markets | Vendor: Transport & Logistics |
| Cooperative | Smallholder group running shared crops, livestock and marketing | Farm workspace |

A blue note says individual buyers do not need a business account. Press **Continue**, or **Back** to return.

### Step 3: Your Business

The heading is **Tell us about your business**. It sets the name, location and currency across the platform.

| Field | Required? | Choices |
|---|---|---|
| Business Name | Yes | Free text. Example: Sunrise Poultry Farm. If you leave it empty you see **Business name is required**. |
| Country | No (starts on Zambia) | Zambia, South Africa, Nigeria, Kenya, Tanzania, Uganda, Zimbabwe, Malawi, Mozambique, Ghana, Cameroon, Ethiopia, Rwanda, DR Congo, Botswana, Namibia, Senegal, Côte d'Ivoire, Other |
| Currency | No (starts on ZMW) | ZMW, USD, ZAR, KES, NGN, GHS, TZS, UGX, ETB, XOF |

Press **Continue**. At this moment the workspace is created, and your business type, country and currency are also saved to your company account.

**What the name, country and currency change afterwards:** the business name appears under the logo in the sidebar and as the round initial at the top right. The currency is shown in the top bar and used wherever money is shown. The workspace timezone is set to Africa/Lusaka.

> **Watch out:** The business name you type here is the name of your workspace. It does not automatically copy the name you gave at registration, so type it again exactly as you want it to appear.

### Step 4a: Setup for Farmers and Cooperatives

The heading is **What does your farm operate?** You do two things: choose categories, then add at least one **enterprise**.

An **enterprise** is one line of business on your farm, for example "Broilers", "Maize" or "Tilapia ponds". Each enterprise is built from a **template**, a ready-made production plan with stages, record forms and care guides (see Part 2 — Templates, Record Fields and Care Guides).

**Step by step**

1. Press every **category card** that fits your farm. A tick marks the choice. You can pick several.
2. Under the cards, a panel appears for each category you picked, listing its templates. Press a template to add it as an enterprise. You can press the same template again to add a second one.
3. Each added enterprise appears in the green box **Added enterprises** with a name like "Broiler Chickens 1". Click in the name to rename it. Press the small **✕** to remove it.
4. When you have at least one enterprise, press **Finish setup**.

**The 14 category cards**

| Category | Examples shown |
|---|---|
| Poultry & Birds | Chicken, duck, turkey, guinea fowl, quail, goose, ostrich, pigeon |
| Livestock | Cattle, goats, sheep, pigs, rabbits, horses, camels |
| Aquaculture | Tilapia, catfish, trout, carp, shrimp, aquaponics |
| Crops & Grains | Maize, wheat, rice, soybean, millet, sorghum, cassava, yam, sugarcane |
| Horticulture | Tomato, onion, pepper, carrot, kale, watermelon, garlic, eggplant |
| Greenhouse | Cut roses, herbs, hydroponic lettuce, bell pepper, strawberry |
| Orchards & Tree Fruit | Mango, avocado, citrus, banana, guava, pawpaw, passion fruit, macadamia |
| Apiary & Beekeeping | Beekeeping, honey, wax, pollination services |
| Mushroom Cultivation | Oyster, shiitake, button, portobello |
| Insects & Worms | Black soldier fly, crickets, mealworms, silkworms, vermiculture, snails, stingless bees |
| Aquatic Plants & Algae | Seaweed farming, spirulina, microalgae |
| Forestry & Agroforestry | Timber plantations, agroforestry, silvopastoral systems |
| Agro-Processing | Milling, dairy, honey, cassava, juice, feed mill, meat processing |
| Agricultural Services | Vet services, consulting, equipment hire, lab testing, farmer training |

**Messages you may see**

- **Select at least one category to continue.** (amber text) — pick a category card.
- **Add at least one enterprise above to continue.** (amber text) — press a template. The **Finish setup** button stays grey until you do.

### Step 5: Ready (Farmers and Cooperatives)

A green tick and the heading **You're all set!** show how many enterprises were created and list them with their template names. Press **Open my workspace**. The wizard ends and the farm workspace opens on the Dashboard.

### Step 4b: Setup for vendors

The heading is **What do you offer?** You tick the products or services your business provides. Press **Finish setup** once at least one is ticked (otherwise you see **Select at least one to continue.**). There is no "Ready" screen: the vendor workspace opens immediately.

| Business type | Options offered |
|---|---|
| Agro Dealer | Seeds, Fertilizers, Pesticides, Animal Feeds, Farming Tools, Irrigation Supplies |
| Vet / Animal Health | General Vet Consults, Vaccination Programs, Surgery, Lab Diagnostics, Farm Visits, Livestock Nutrition |
| Equipment Hire | Tractors, Ploughing, Planters, Harvesters, Irrigation Rigs, Spray Equipment |
| AgriFood Seller | Grains & Cereals, Vegetables, Fruits, Poultry & Eggs, Meat & Fish, Dairy Products |
| AgriSupply Provider | Packaging Materials, Cold Chain Logistics, Transport & Delivery, Storage Solutions, Farm Chemicals |
| AgriServices Provider | Land Preparation, Irrigation Installation, Fencing & Security, Soil & Water Testing, Consulting |
| Agro Processor | Grain Milling, Oil Pressing, Dairy Processing, Meat Processing, Drying & Packaging, Fermentation |
| Transport / Haulage | Grain Haulage, Livestock Transport, Refrigerated Transport, Input Delivery, Last-mile Delivery, Cross-border Freight |

Your ticks are saved with the company workspace as your list of service categories.

### What each choice changes afterwards

| Choice | What it controls |
|---|---|
| Business type | Whether you see the farm workspace or a vendor workspace, and which tabs the vendor workspace has. It is also how government and programme dashboards count you (farm, vet, dealer and so on). |
| Business name | The name shown in the sidebar and top bar. |
| Country and currency | The currency shown in the top bar and on money screens. |
| Categories and enterprises (farms) | Which production pages and templates your farm starts with. Each enterprise opens its own production page with cycles, stages and records. |
| Service categories (vendors) | A record of what you offer. Your public listing and services are managed in the vendor tabs (see Parts 6 and 7). |

### Changing your set-up later

- **Add more enterprises:** at any time from **Settings**, press **Add Enterprise** (see the Settings chapter), or add them from the Production page.
- **Business name, country, currency, business type:** the **Business Profile** card in Settings only *shows* these. There is no edit button. The only way to change them is **Reset workspace** (below), which erases the workspace and runs the wizard again.
- **Vendor service categories:** there is no screen to re-tick them. Keep your services up to date in your vendor tabs instead.

### Reset workspace and its warning

**Reset workspace (re-run onboarding)** is in the **Danger Zone** at the bottom of **Settings**. It is shown only to the **director**, and only in the farm workspace (vendor workspaces do not have this button).

What the warning says: *"This will erase the whole company workspace for every member and restart onboarding. This action cannot be undone."* When you press the button, the browser asks **"Are you sure? This will reset the entire workspace for everyone in your company and cannot be undone."**

**What it erases:** the shared workspace — business profile, enterprises, production cycles with their stages, daily records and events, the care-guide ticks, your company's saved templates ("My templates") and the edit-approval settings and queue. It also clears the copy kept in your browser and reloads the page, and then the wizard starts again for everybody.

**What it does not erase:** the Finance ledger, Sales (customers, orders, payments) and Inventory are stored separately in the company account; the Reset button does not touch them. Team members, roles, branches and permissions stay too. Items kept only in a browser (see "Where your data is kept") are not erased on other devices.

> **Watch out:** Reset removes every production cycle and record the whole company has entered. Use **Backup & Restore** only for browser-only items; it does **not** back up production cycles. If you only want to correct a name, think twice before using Reset.

## Finding your way around

### The farm workspace (Farmers and Cooperatives)

The farm workspace has a dark **sidebar** on the left, a white **top bar** and the main page area.

**The sidebar** has the logo and your business name at the top, then four labelled groups, then the bottom items.

- On a large screen the sidebar is always there. The **Collapse** button at the very bottom shrinks it to a thin strip of icons; hover over an icon to see its name. Press the arrow again to widen it.
- On a phone or narrow window the sidebar is hidden. Press the **menu icon** (three lines) at the top left of the top bar to slide it in. A dark layer covers the page; press the layer, or choose a page, and the sidebar slides away again. The Collapse button is not shown on small screens.
- You only see the pages your role allows. If you cannot see a menu item, ask your administrator (see "Team and Permissions").

**The top bar** shows the name of the current page, a **bell** (see "Notifications"), your currency (hidden on very small screens) and a round badge with the first letter of your business name. The badge is not a menu.

**Every menu item**

| Group / item | What it is for | Explained in |
|---|---|---|
| OPERATIONS | | |
| Dashboard | Overview of the farm, shortcuts to enterprises | Part 3 |
| Production | Your enterprises, cycles, stages, daily records, events | Part 3 |
| Processing | Recipes and batches that turn raw produce into finished goods | Part 3 |
| Inventory | Stock items, movements, alerts | Part 4 |
| Sales | Customers, orders, payments | Part 4 |
| Trade & Export | Contracts, buyers, export documents | Part 4 |
| Finance | Income and expense ledger | Part 4 |
| Credit Profile | A credit score and a shareable lender report | Part 4 |
| Reports | Business reports and charts | Part 4 |
| MARKETPLACES | | |
| AgriFood Market | Post and browse food listings | Part 7 |
| AgriSupply | Post and browse farm-supply listings | Part 7 |
| AgriServices | Post and browse service listings | Part 6 |
| Agro Dealers | Directory of input dealers | Part 7 |
| Equipment Hire | Directory of equipment owners, bookings | Part 6 |
| Vet Services | Directory of vets, appointments | Part 6 |
| AI & INTELLIGENCE | | |
| Smart Engine | Alerts and recommendations drawn from your data | Part 5 |
| AI Insights | Predictions | Part 5 |
| Financial AI | Money insights | Part 5 |
| Smart Vision | Image and camera tools | Part 5 |
| Weather | Forecasts, fields, satellite plant-health readings | Part 5 |
| Sustainability | Sustainability tracking | Part 5 |
| Smart Devices | Connected sensors and gadgets | Part 5 |
| Water Mgmt, Aquaculture, Poultry Houses, Energy, Soil Depth, Automation | Device dashboards and rules | Part 5 |
| Device Gateway, Cold Chain, Farm Gateway | Gateways and cold-room monitoring | Part 5 |
| FARM MANAGEMENT | | |
| Notifications | Alert centre for device alerts | This part and Part 5 |
| Animal Health | Vaccinations, treatments, vet visits | Part 5 |
| Staff & Labour | Farm staff and time logs | Part 5 |
| Procurement | Suppliers and purchase orders | Part 5 |
| Requisitions | Internal requests for items | Part 5 |
| Activity Log | Scheduled activities | Part 5 |
| Crop Rotation | Fields and rotation planning | Part 5 |
| Bottom of the sidebar | | |
| Team & Permissions | People, roles, branches, audit log | This part |
| Settings | Business profile, consent, approval control, danger zone | This part |
| Backup & Restore | Download or restore browser-only data | This part |
| Collapse | Shrinks the sidebar (desktop only) | This part |

**How the menu follows your permissions:** each menu item is tied to a module (for example Inventory belongs to the Feed & Inventory module). If your role cannot **view** that module, the menu item is hidden. Dashboard, Notifications and Backup & Restore are always shown. Team & Permissions is shown only to administrators (or to people whom an administrator has given view access to it). The table of modules and roles is in the Team chapter.

> **Tip:** If a colleague tells you about a page you cannot see, it is almost certainly a permission, not a fault. Ask the director to open **Team & Permissions** and check your access.

### The vendor workspace (every other business type)

The vendor workspace has a sidebar of **tabs** and a main area. There is no top bar with a bell.

- The three-line button at the top of the sidebar widens or narrows the sidebar. Narrowed, you see only icons.
- The sidebar stays on screen on a phone. Press the three-line button to narrow it to icons so the page has more room.
- The top of the main area shows your type and the current tab, and your district (or "Set your district in Settings").
- **Sign Out** is at the bottom of the sidebar.
- While your business workspace is being prepared you may see **Setting up your business workspace…** for a moment.
- If a tab runs into trouble you see **This tab hit an error: …. Try another tab or reload.** Other tabs keep working.

Every vendor type has an **Overview** tab first and these tabs at the end: **Clients**, **Invoices**, **Revenue**, **Marketplace Listing**, **Users & Permissions** (only for people who may manage the team) and **Settings**. The tabs in between depend on your type:

| Vendor type | Tabs between Overview and Clients |
|---|---|
| Vet Services | Appointments, Patient Records, Prescriptions, Services Catalog |
| Equipment Hire | Fleet Management, Bookings, Maintenance |
| Transport & Logistics | Transport Jobs, Vehicles, Maintenance |
| Agro Dealer | Product Catalog, Orders, Stock Management |
| AgriSupply | Supply Catalog, Purchase Orders, Stock Management, Deliveries |
| AgriFood Seller | Produce Listings, Buyer Orders, Pricing & Stock |
| AgriServices | Services Catalog, Job Schedule |
| Agro Processor | Farm Sourcing, Processing Logs, Inventory, Traceability, Orders |

Parts 6 and 7 explain each of these tabs. This part explains the last two: **Users & Permissions** (the same team page described below) and **Settings** (described below).

### The individual customer page

Individual accounts do not have a business workspace. The customer page has a top bar with **Home**, **Marketplace**, **Saved** and **Profile**, your first name, and **Sign out**. On a phone these sit behind the menu icon.

| Page | What it shows |
|---|---|
| Home | A greeting with your name and email, **Browse Marketplace** and **Saved** buttons, three counters (Saved, Browsed, Calls) and four featured verified providers |
| Marketplace | A similar set of sample listings to the public page, with search and category pills (All, Vet Services, Equipment Hire, Agro Dealers, AgriFood, AgriSupply, AgriServices) |
| Saved | Listings you marked with the heart icon, or "Nothing saved yet" |
| Profile | Your name, **Email**, **Phone**, **Account type** (Individual / Customer), a tip about registering a farm account, and **Sign out** |

On a card, the heart saves or removes a favourite, **Call** phones the provider and **Details** opens a window with the full listing.

> **Watch out:** The saved hearts are kept only while the page is open. If you reload or sign in again, the list is empty. The **Order** and **Enquire** buttons in the detail window do not place a real order yet; use **Call directly** to contact the seller.

### The Government, Extension and Partner pages

These accounts have their own sidebar, with **Sign Out** at the bottom. Tabs are shown only when the person's role includes them.

| Account | Sidebar tabs |
|---|---|
| Government | National Overview, Ecosystem, National Map, Production, Markets & Prices, Disease & Alerts, Climate & Sustainability, District Registry, Advisories, Programmes, Team & Branches |
| Extension officer / supervisor | My Caseload, Visits & Follow-ups, Add Farms, Programmes, Team & Branches |
| Partner | Programmes, then (under "District data") National Overview, Ecosystem, National Map, Production, Markets & Prices, Disease & Alerts, Climate & Sustainability, District Registry, Advisories, and Team & Branches (under "Admin") |

Part 8 explains these. **Team & Branches** is the same team page described in this part.

## Settings (farm workspace)

**What it is for:** Settings shows your business profile, lets the director control data sharing, shows your extension officer and programmes, lists your enterprises, controls edit approval, and holds the Danger Zone. The page is titled **Platform Settings**.

**Before you start:** Settings is shown to roles that have view access to the Settings module. Directors have it; Production Manager, Finance Manager, Sales Manager and Supervisor have view access by default. Some cards can only be changed by the director.

**Step by step:** press **Settings** in the sidebar (at the bottom). Scroll down through the cards in the order below.

### Business Profile

A read-only card with **Name**, **Country**, **Currency** and **Timezone** (Africa/Lusaka). To change these, see "Changing your set-up later".

### Share with government & partners

This card is described fully in the chapter "Data-sharing consent". It also contains your **Province** and **District** boxes and the **Household profile**.

### Your extension officer

Shows the government extension officer assigned to your farm: name, their organisation, and clickable phone and email. If none is assigned you see: *No extension officer is assigned yet. Officers in your district can add your farm once data sharing is enabled and your province / district are set.* If an officer has visited you, a **Visit log** lists the last ten visits with the purpose, date, officer, **Findings**, **Advice** and any planned follow-up date.

### Development programmes

This card appears only when your farm has been enrolled in a programme. For each programme it shows the name, the partner, the funder, a status tag (such as Active), the cohort, and a list of **Support received** (type, description, reference, date and value in the programme's currency) with a running total.

### Enterprises

The card heading shows how many enterprises you have, for example **Enterprises (3)**. Each row shows the icon, name, template name, location (if any) and a green dot (active) or grey dot (inactive). This list is for reference; to edit an enterprise, go to **Production**. If you have none, it says **No enterprises yet. Add one above.**

**Add Enterprise** (green button):

1. Press **Add Enterprise**. A window opens: *Choose a template and name your operation*.
2. Type the **Enterprise Name** (required). Example: Mpongwe Fish Ponds.
3. Choose a **Production Template** (required). Use the category buttons (All, Poultry & Birds, Livestock, Aquaculture, Crops, Horticulture, Greenhouse, Orchard, Apiary, Mushroom, Processing, Services) or the search box. Your own saved templates come first and carry a **My template** badge.
4. Optionally add a **Location** (example: Plot 5, Kabwe Road) and **Notes**.
5. Press **Add Enterprise**. The button briefly says **Saved!** and the window closes.

The footer shows the template you selected, or **No template selected**. If nothing matches the search, you see **No templates match your search**. The **Add Enterprise** button stays grey until both a name and a template are chosen.

> **Note:** The category buttons in this window do not include Insects & Worms, Aquatic Plants & Algae or Forestry & Agroforestry. Use **All** or the search box to find those templates.

### Edit & Approval Control

This card lets the company require a second person to approve changes to production records. It is saved with the company workspace, so it applies on every device.

**What you will see**

| Control | What it does |
|---|---|
| Require approval for edits (switch) | When on, edits and deletions of production records, events and cycles are held as "pending" until an approver accepts them. When off, edits apply immediately. |
| Your name / Staff member | A free-text name used on the requests (example: John Banda) |
| Approver name | The name of the person who approves (example: Farm Manager). It is shown as "reviewed by" on decisions. |
| Require a reason for every edit or deletion (tick-box) | Makes the edit windows ask for a reason |

A yellow tag with a clock shows how many requests are pending. A **Settings** badge in the sidebar also shows the pending number.

**Pending Edits:** each request shows what is being changed, the field, who asked, the **Before** and **After** values (trimmed to 80 characters) and the **Reason**. Press **Approve** to apply the change, or **Reject** to open a small window: type an optional **Reason for rejection** and press **Reject** (or **Cancel**).

**Recent Decisions:** the last ten approved (green) and rejected (red) requests, with who asked, who reviewed and any rejection note. The small **✕** on a row removes it from the list.

> **Watch out:** The names are typed text, not logins. The page does not check *who* is pressing **Approve**: anyone who can open Settings can approve a pending request. Keep Settings access limited to people you trust, using Team & Permissions.

### Danger Zone

Shown to directors only. See "Reset workspace and its warning" above.

### Settings in a vendor workspace

In a vendor workspace the **Settings** tab holds the same **Share with government & partners** card (data sharing, province and district, household profile) followed by a **Business profile** form and a **Team** list.

**Business profile form**

| Field | Notes |
|---|---|
| Business name * | Required |
| Marketplace category | The group your listing appears under (Agro Dealer / Input Supplier, Equipment Hire, Veterinary Services, Agro Processing, Transport & Logistics, Agricultural Finance, Crop / Livestock Insurance, Extension & Advisory Services, Cold Storage, Certification & Inspection, AgriFood Seller / Fresh Produce, Agricultural Services) |
| Tagline | One line customers see first |
| Description | Longer text |
| Phone, WhatsApp, Email, Website | Contact details |
| District, Town, Address | Where you are. Your district is also shown at the top of the workspace. |
| Districts served | Comma-separated, for example Chongwe, Kafue, Chilanga |
| Specialties | Comma-separated, for example Poultry, dairy, maize seed |
| Service hours | Example: Mon-Fri 08:00-17:00 |
| Year established | A number |
| Business registration no., TPIN / tax id | Your registration and tax numbers |
| We travel to the farm (tick) | Shows you offer mobile service |
| Emergency call-outs available (tick) | Shows you take emergencies |

Press **Save profile**. You see **Business profile saved.** or the error message.

**Team (vendor)** is a simple list of your people (name, role, phone, licence, active) with **+ Add person**, **Edit** and **Remove**. Roles: Owner / Director, Branch Manager, Veterinary Officer, Agronomist, Sales Representative, Technician, Driver / Operator, Other. These people are **staff records for assigning work** (for example to appointments and jobs). They do not get logins. To give someone a login, use **Users & Permissions**.

## Team and Permissions

**What it is for:** this is where an administrator adds people to the company, gives each person a role, decides exactly what each person may see and change, groups people into branches, and reviews a record of every access change. It works the same way for farms, vendors, government offices and partner organisations. The roles and the list of modules change with the type of organisation.

**Where to find it**

| Account | Where |
|---|---|
| Farm / Cooperative | **Team & Permissions** at the bottom of the sidebar |
| Vendor | **Users & Permissions** tab |
| Government, Extension, Partner | **Team & Branches** tab |

**Before you start**

- Only an **administrator** can add or change people. An administrator is the person who registered the company, anyone with the **Director**, **Administrator** (government or partner) role, and anyone an administrator has marked as an administrator.
- Other people can open the page only if they have been given view access to "Team & Permissions". They see everything in read-only form. The heading then says **read-only (you are not an administrator)**.
- The header line shows your organisation, the number of active members and the number of active branches.

The page has three tabs: **Members**, **Branches** and **Audit log**.

### Members

**What you will see:** a table with these columns.

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Member | Name, with a green shield if they are an administrator and "(you)" next to your own name. Below the name: their email and phone (or just the phone if they have no email). |
| Role | Their role. Administrators can change it with a drop-down (not on their own row). |
| Branch | Their branch, or **Org-wide**. Administrators can change it with a drop-down. |
| Access | **Full access** for administrators; otherwise "N of M modules" they can view. A yellow **custom** tag appears when the person has their own permission settings instead of the role's default. |
| Status | **Active** or **Deactivated** |
| (buttons) | Shield: edit permissions. Green shield: make or remove administrator. Person icon: deactivate or reactivate |

Tick **Show deactivated** above the table to include people who have been switched off.

### Adding a person

1. Press **Add member**. A form opens.
2. Type **Full name** (required).
3. Type **Phone** (required). The hint says "Phone (login if no email)".
4. Optionally type **Email**. Give an email whenever you can: it makes sign-in simpler.
5. Pick a **Role** from the list. A line beside the form describes what the chosen role does.
6. Pick a **Branch**, or leave **Organisation-wide (no branch)**.
7. Optionally type **Set a password** (at least 6 characters). If you leave it empty, the platform makes up a 10-character password.
8. Press **Add member** (the button shows "Adding…" while it works).

**What happens next**

- A yellow box appears: **Temporary password for (name)**, showing the **Login** (email, or phone if no email) and the **Password**. It is shown **once**. Press the copy icon to copy both, then send them privately (for example by WhatsApp message to the person only). Press **X** to dismiss the box.
- A green message confirms: "(Name) added as (Role)."
- The person gets a welcome notice inside the platform. If you gave a real email address and the platform's email service is switched on, they are also emailed their login and temporary password.
- The new person can sign in at once. They cannot change their password from inside the app today.

If the person already exists the form shows a red message:

| Message | Meaning |
|---|---|
| A user with this phone number already exists in your organisation. | That phone number is already used by someone in your company. |
| A user with this email already exists. | That email is used elsewhere on the platform. |
| Password must be at least 6 characters. | Choose a longer password. |
| Invalid role for a farm organisation. Allowed: … | The chosen role does not belong to this organisation type. |
| Branch not found | The branch was removed. Refresh and pick another. |
| Permission denied: only an organisation administrator can add team members | You are not an administrator. |

### Changing a role, branch or administrator status

- **Role:** choose a new one from the Role drop-down on the person's row. A green message says "(Name) is now (Role)." You cannot change your own role.
- **Branch:** choose from the Branch drop-down. The message says "(Name) moved to (Branch)." or "…organisation-wide."
- **Administrator:** press the green shield on the row (not on your own row). The message says "(Name) is now an administrator." or "(Name) is no longer an administrator."

Rules and error messages:

| Message | Meaning |
|---|---|
| You cannot remove your own administrator access | Ask another administrator to do it. |
| An organisation must keep at least one administrator | Make someone else an administrator first. |
| director is an administrator role; change the role instead | A Director is always an administrator. To remove admin rights, change their role first. |
| Administrators always have full access; remove admin first to restrict them | You cannot edit the permissions of an administrator. |
| Invalid role. | The role is not valid for this organisation. |

### Deactivating and reactivating

Press the person icon on the row. For deactivating you are asked **"Deactivate (name)? They will no longer be able to sign in."** Their records stay. They are listed as **Deactivated** (dimmed) when **Show deactivated** is ticked. Press the person icon again to reactivate. You cannot deactivate yourself (**You cannot deactivate yourself**). There is no way to delete a person.

### The roles for each organisation type

**Farms and vendors (every Farm, Cooperative and vendor company)**

| Role | Description shown | In plain words |
|---|---|---|
| Director / Owner | Full access, manages the team | Does everything. Is always an administrator. |
| Production Manager | Runs production, inventory and procurement | Edits production, inventory, procurement, biosecurity; views reports and settings |
| Finance Manager | Finance, sales, credit profile, reports | Edits finance, sales, inventory, procurement, credit profile; views reports and trade documents |
| Sales Manager | Sales, marketplace, trade documents | Edits sales, trade documents, marketplace; views stock and reports |
| Supervisor | Day-to-day production records | Edits production records, inventory and biosecurity; views procurement and devices |
| Farmhand | Logs daily records | Can add (not change) production records; views the dashboard and planner |
| Veterinary Officer | Animal health and biosecurity | Edits biosecurity and vet records; can add production records |
| Driver | Mobile app, deliveries | Views the dashboard, planner and mobile app only |

When you add a person, the role list starts on **Driver** (the last one), so check it before you press **Add member**.

**Government offices**

| Role | Description shown |
|---|---|
| Administrator | Manages the team, branches and advisories; sees everything |
| Dashboard viewer | Read-only district & national dashboards |
| Extension supervisor | Oversees officers in the branch, issues advisories |
| Extension officer | Caseload of farms, visits, direct advice |

**NGO / development partner and donor programmes**

| Role | Description shown |
|---|---|
| Administrator | Manages team, branches and programmes |
| Programme manager | Runs programmes: enrolment, support, notices |
| M&E officer | Records indicator readings and views results |
| Observer (donor / auditor) | Read-only access to programmes and dashboards |

A person's role must match their organisation. A farm cannot be given a government role, and a government office cannot be given a farm role.

### The module permission matrix

Every organisation has a list of **modules**: the areas of the platform that can be switched on or off for each person. Each module has four actions:

| Action | What it lets the person do |
|---|---|
| View | See the module (its menu item shows; without View, it is hidden) |
| Create | Add new records |
| Edit | Change existing records |
| Delete | Remove records |

Creating, editing or deleting always needs View too. The platform enforces this on the server as well as on the screen, so a hidden button is not the only protection.

**Farm and vendor modules (27)**

| Group | Modules |
|---|---|
| Overview | Dashboard, Smart AI Engine, Daily Planner |
| Production | Broiler Poultry, Village Chicken, Piggery, Fish Farming, Duck Management, Goats & Sheep, Horticulture, Production Records |
| Operations | Feed & Inventory, Sales & Customers, Financial Management, Credit Profile, Human Resources, Procurement, Biosecurity & Vet, Weather & Fields, Smart Devices & IoT |
| Strategy | Reports & BI, Trade & Export, Marketplace, Assets & Infra, Mobile App, Settings, Team & Permissions |

**Which sidebar item follows which module (farm workspace)**

| Sidebar item | Needs View on |
|---|---|
| Production, Processing, Smart Vision | Production Records |
| Inventory | Feed & Inventory |
| Sales | Sales & Customers |
| Trade & Export | Trade & Export |
| Finance, Financial AI | Financial Management |
| Credit Profile | Credit Profile |
| Reports, Sustainability | Reports & BI |
| AgriFood Market, AgriSupply, AgriServices, Agro Dealers, Equipment Hire, Vet Services | Marketplace |
| Smart Engine, AI Insights | Smart AI Engine |
| Weather | Weather & Fields |
| Smart Devices, Water Mgmt, Aquaculture, Poultry Houses, Energy, Soil Depth, Automation, Device Gateway, Cold Chain, Farm Gateway | Smart Devices & IoT |
| Animal Health | Biosecurity & Vet |
| Staff & Labour | Human Resources |
| Procurement, Requisitions | Procurement |
| Crop Rotation | Daily Planner |
| Settings | Settings |
| Team & Permissions | Team & Permissions |

The Dashboard, Notifications, Activity Log and Backup & Restore items are not tied to a module and are shown to everyone.

**What each farm role can do by default**

| Role | View only | View, add and change (no delete) | View and add only / full |
|---|---|---|---|
| Director | | | Everything, including delete (full access) |
| Production Manager | Dashboard, Smart AI Engine, Reports & BI, Settings, Weather & Fields, Smart Devices & IoT, Marketplace | Daily Planner, all eight production modules, Feed & Inventory, Procurement, Biosecurity & Vet | |
| Finance Manager | Dashboard, Daily Planner, Reports & BI, Trade & Export, Settings, Marketplace | Financial Management, Sales & Customers, Feed & Inventory, Procurement, Credit Profile | |
| Sales Manager | Dashboard, Daily Planner, Feed & Inventory, Reports & BI, Settings, Weather & Fields | Sales & Customers, Trade & Export, Marketplace | |
| Supervisor | Dashboard, Daily Planner, Procurement, Settings, Weather & Fields, Smart Devices & IoT | Eight production modules, Feed & Inventory, Biosecurity & Vet | |
| Farmhand | Dashboard, Daily Planner, Mobile App, Weather & Fields | | Eight production modules |
| Veterinary Officer | Dashboard, Daily Planner, Mobile App | Biosecurity & Vet | Eight production modules |
| Driver | Dashboard, Daily Planner, Mobile App | | |

"Eight production modules" means Broiler Poultry, Village Chicken, Piggery, Fish Farming, Duck Management, Goats & Sheep, Horticulture and Production Records. Only the Director (and other administrators) can **delete**.

**Government modules:** District & national dashboards, District registry, Advisories, Extension caseload, Visits & follow-ups, Programmes, Participant enrolment, Support ledger, M&E indicators, Programme notices, Team & Permissions, Organisation settings.

| Government role | By default |
|---|---|
| Administrator | Everything, including the team |
| Dashboard viewer | View only: dashboards, registry, programmes, enrolment, support ledger, M&E |
| Extension supervisor | View: dashboards, registry, settings. Full control of caseload and visits. View and add advisories. View, add and change programmes, enrolment, support, M&E and notices |
| Extension officer | View: dashboards, registry, programmes, enrolment, support. View, add and change caseload and visits. View and add advisories |

**Partner modules:** Programmes, Farm enrolment, Support ledger, M&E indicators, Programme notices, District & national dashboards, District registry, Advisories, Team & Permissions, Organisation settings.

| Partner role | By default |
|---|---|
| Administrator | Everything, including the team |
| Programme manager | Full control of programmes, enrolment, support, M&E and notices. View dashboards, registry, settings. View and add advisories |
| M&E officer | View programmes, enrolment, support, dashboards, registry, settings. View, add and change M&E indicators |
| Observer | View only: programmes, enrolment, support, M&E, notices, dashboards, registry |

### Giving one person different permissions (per-person overrides)

Roles are starting points. You can give one person a custom set without changing the role.

**Step by step**

1. In the **Members** tab, press the **shield** icon on the person's row (administrators do not have this icon, because they always have full access).
2. A window titled **(name) — module permissions** opens, with a table. Rows are the modules, grouped under headings; columns are **View**, **Create**, **Edit**, **Delete** and **All**.
3. Tick or untick boxes. Ticking Create, Edit or Delete ticks View for you. Unticking View clears the other three. The **all** / **none** link at the end of a row ticks or clears the whole row.
4. A small amber dot beside a module name means it differs from the role's default.
5. Press **Save permissions**. A green message says "Permissions saved for (name)."

To go back to the role default, open the same window and press **Reset to role default**. The message says "(Name) reset to the (Role) default." The yellow **custom** tag disappears.

> **Good to know:** a saved custom set **replaces** the role default completely. Anything you leave unticked is off, even if the role would normally allow it. If you later change the person's role, their custom set stays until you reset it.

> **Tip:** To make a read-only accountant, add them as a Finance Manager, then open permissions and untick Create and Edit on the modules they should only view.

### Branches

A **branch** is a site or office of your organisation. Branches let you group people and, for government and partner organisations, limit what people see to a place.

**What you will see:** the list of branches, each with its name, an **HQ** tag if it is the headquarters, its code, its kind, district and province, address and number of members. A dimmed row is deactivated. If you have none it says **No branches yet — the whole organisation acts as one unit.**

**Branch kinds**

| Organisation | Kinds you can choose |
|---|---|
| Farm / vendor | Farm site, Warehouse / pack-house, Sales outlet, Office |
| Government | Headquarters, Provincial office, District office (DACO), Agricultural camp, Research station |
| NGO / Donor | Country office, Field office, Project site |

**Add a branch**

1. Press **Add branch**.
2. Type the **Branch name** (required; names must be different within your organisation).
3. Choose the **kind**.
4. Optionally fill **Code**, **Province**, **District**, **Address** and **Phone**.
5. Optionally tick **Headquarters — members here are organisation-wide, not scoped to a district**. Only one branch can be the headquarters: ticking it on one branch unticks it on the previous one.
6. Press **Create branch**. A message says "(Name) created."

Each row has **Edit**, **Deactivate** / **Reactivate** and, when the branch has no members, **Delete** (after a confirmation). If a branch still has members you see **Move or deactivate the members of this branch first**.

**What branches do**

- **Farm and vendor:** "Farm sites, pack-houses and outlets. Members can be assigned to a site." They are labels for organising your people.
- **Government:** "HQ, provincial and district offices, camps. Members of a district office or camp only see and act on their own district; provincial members see their province; HQ sees the nation." So set the **Province** and **District** on the branch.
- **NGO / Donor:** "Country office and field offices / project sites."
- People with no branch, or in the HQ branch, are organisation-wide.

### Audit log

An administrator-only tab. Everyone else sees **Only administrators can view the audit log.** It lists the 100 most recent access changes: date and time, what happened, who did it, and who it was done to, with details.

| Entry | Recorded when |
|---|---|
| Member Invited | A person was added (role and branch shown) |
| Role Changed | A role was changed |
| Permissions Changed / Permissions Reset | A custom set was saved / removed |
| Member Deactivated / Member Activated | A person was switched off / on |
| Member Branch Changed | A person moved branch |
| Admin Granted / Admin Revoked | Administrator status changed |
| Branch Created / Branch Updated / Branch Deleted | A branch changed |

If nothing has happened yet it says **No changes recorded yet.**

### Who can do what (summary)

| Task | Who |
|---|---|
| See the Team page | Administrators, and anyone given View on Team & Permissions (read-only) |
| Add, deactivate, change role, branch, permissions, make admin | Administrators only |
| Create, edit, delete branches | Administrators only |
| View the audit log | Administrators only |
| Change data-sharing consent | The Director only |
| Reset workspace | The Director only (farm workspace) |

## Data-sharing consent

**What it is for:** your farm's figures can help the Ministry of Agriculture, extension services and development partners understand what is happening in a district. Sharing is **your choice** and is **off** until you switch it on.

**Where:** **Settings**, card **Share with government & partners** (farm workspace), or the **Settings** tab (vendor workspace). It is not shown to government or partner accounts.

**Before you start:** only the **Director** can turn it on or off. Everyone else sees the switch greyed out; hover over it and it says "Only a director can change this". If someone else tries, the server answers **Permission denied: only a director can change data-sharing consent**.

**Step by step**

1. Open Settings and find **Share with government & partners**.
2. Press the round switch on the right (green = on, grey = off). A line below says **Sharing enabled.** or **Sharing disabled.**
3. When it is on, the card also says "Sharing since (date)".
4. Fill your **Province** (example Lusaka) and **District** (example Chongwe) and press **Save**. The **Save** button wakes up once you change a box. The message says **Location saved.**

### What gets shared

The card says: when enabled, "this organisation's counts (enterprises, records, prices, reports) are included in de-identified district and national dashboards used by the Ministry of Agriculture, extension services and development partners. Your organisation's name is never shown. You also receive district advisories."

| Who | What they see |
|---|---|
| Government (dashboard viewers, administrators) | Totals and averages for farms and cooperatives that opted in, per district or nationally. No farm names. |
| Extension officers | Farms that opted in and are in their district can be added to their caseload, so you need consent on **and** your province and district filled in. |
| Partners | Dashboards like government, and they can enrol farms that opted in into programmes. |
| Lenders | A lender never gets access through consent. A lender sees your **Credit Profile** only if you create a share link and send it to them (see Part 4). The profile does show whether you have given consent, which counts in your favour. |

Some figures do not depend on consent: counts of how many organisations of each type exist, and the public marketplace listings that are already visible to everyone.

### What switching it off does

When you switch sharing off, your counts are left out of dashboards from that moment on, and the "Sharing since" line disappears.

> **Watch out:** The province and district you enter place you on district dashboards and decide which extension officers can find you. If they are wrong, your numbers appear in the wrong place.

### Household profile

Under the consent switch is a smaller section, **Household profile**. Programmes you join report results by sex, age and household size (for example "women-headed farms trained"). The director can press **Add profile** (or **Edit**) and fill in:

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Head of household | Sex: Female, Male, Other / prefer not to say, or not recorded |
| Year of birth | Used to work out age and whether the head is a youth (35 or younger) |
| Household size | Number of people |
| Land (ha) | Land area in hectares |
| Female members, Male members | Counts |
| National ID (stored hashed) | Optional; kept only as a scrambled code so duplicates can be spotted |
| Household includes a person with a disability | Tick-box |

Press **Save profile**. The message is **Household profile saved.** Only counts are ever shared. Until you fill it in the card says **Not recorded yet.**

## Backup & Restore

**What it is for:** a safety copy of the information that is kept **only in this browser**. The page is titled **Data Backup & Restore**. It is available to every signed-in farm user.

> **Watch out:** this backup does **not** contain your Finance ledger, Sales, Inventory, production cycles and records, team or vendor data. Those live in your company account and are safe without a backup. Likewise, a restore does not bring back deleted company data. The page's own wording is: "Your Finance ledger, Sales, Inventory, production cycles and records are stored in your company account, not in this file."

### What is saved in the backup file

Everything the farm workspace keeps in this browser only, for example: device dashboards (aquaculture ponds, water, poultry houses, energy, soil) and their alerts; Smart Devices, gateway and cold-chain settings; Animal Health records; Staff & Labour; Procurement and Requisitions; Processing recipes and batches; the Activity Log; Crop Rotation; Smart Vision, Sustainability and Financial AI records; the food, supply and service listings you post in the farm marketplaces; and the last-backup date. The page shows **Keys to back up** (how many separate items) and **Last backup** (date and time, or **Never**).

### What is not saved

- Your sign-in pass.
- Anything already stored in the company account (see the table in "Where your data is kept").
- Items that exist only on another device: a backup holds what *this* browser holds.

> **Note:** The description box at the bottom of the page lists enterprises, production cycles, inventory and finance records as "included". Those are a leftover from an older version. Today they are in the company account, and a restore will not override the company copy.

### Making a backup

1. Open **Backup & Restore** (bottom of the sidebar).
2. Press **Download Backup**.
3. Your browser saves a file whose name starts with "agronexus_backup" and ends with today's date, for example the 18th of October 2026. Keep it somewhere safe, for example on a flash disk or in your email.
4. **Last backup** now shows the time.

Make a backup after each big session of data entry in the browser-only pages, and before you clear your browser, change computers or use a new phone.

### Restoring from a backup

1. Press **Choose file** under **Select backup file** and choose a backup file (it must end in .json). The file name and size appear.
2. Read the warning: **Restoring will OVERWRITE current data. This cannot be undone.**
3. Press **Restore Data**.
4. A green message says **Successfully restored N keys. Reloading page…** and the page reloads by itself.

If the file is not a proper backup you see a red message: either the technical message from the browser, or *Failed to parse backup file. Make sure it is a valid AgroNexus JSON backup.* The **Restore Data** button is grey until you have chosen a file.

> **Tip:** To move browser-only data from an old computer to a new one: make a backup on the old one, sign in on the new one, restore, and check each page.

> **Watch out:** A restore replaces the matching items in *this* browser with what is in the file. If you have entered newer data since the backup, it is lost.

## Notifications and your account menu

### The bell

In the farm workspace the **bell** at the top right shows a red number when you have unresolved alerts from your devices (up to **9+**). Pressing it opens the **Notifications** page. The same page is in the sidebar under Farm Management, with the red number beside it.

The **Notifications Centre** page ("Aggregated alerts from all IoT modules") gathers alerts from Aquaculture, Water, Poultry, Energy, Soil and Devices into one list.

| Control | What it does |
|---|---|
| Refresh | Reloads the alerts |
| Clear Resolved | Removes resolved alerts (appears when there are some) |
| Critical, Warning, Info, Resolved today | Counters; press one to filter the list |
| Severity buttons | all, critical, warning, info |
| Module drop-down | All modules, Aquaculture, Water, Poultry, Energy, Soil, Devices |
| Status drop-down | All statuses, Unresolved, Resolved |

Each alert has a **Resolve** action. An empty list shows **All clear — no active alerts**.

> **Note:** these alerts are kept in this browser only. They are not sent to your phone as messages, and a colleague on another computer sees their own list. See Part 5 for how alerts are created.

There is no bell in the vendor, government, extension or partner workspaces, and no bell for individual customers.

### Your profile and account menu

There is **no account menu** in the farm or vendor workspaces. The round badge at the top right of the farm workspace shows the first letter of your business name and does nothing when pressed. Your own name and phone are shown on the team page, and you can see your login on the **Profile** page only if you are an individual customer.

There is currently no screen to change your name, phone, photo or password. Ask an administrator or AGRINUXES support if something is wrong.

### Plans, subscription and billing

Plans (Starter, Professional, Enterprise), a free-trial counter and an upgrade window were designed, but they are **not connected to the screens you use today**. You will not see a plan page, a trial counter or a payment screen, and no module is locked for lack of payment. If this changes, an announcement will explain it.

## Where your data is kept

Some things you type are saved to your **company account**: every person in the company sees them, on every phone or computer, and they survive if you lose your device. Other things are kept **in this browser only**: only that one browser on that one device sees them, and they can be lost if the browser's data is cleared. Use **Backup & Restore** for the second kind.

| Area | Where it is saved | What that means for you |
|---|---|---|
| Account, team, roles, permissions, branches, audit log | Company account | Shared, safe |
| Business profile, enterprises, production cycles, stages, daily records, events, care-guide ticks | Company account (the farm workspace) | Shared across the team and devices |
| Saved templates ("My templates") | Company account | Shared |
| Edit-approval settings and pending requests | Company account | Shared |
| Finance (income and expense ledger) | Company account | Shared |
| Sales (customers, orders, payments) | Company account | Shared |
| Inventory (stock items and every movement) | Company account | Shared |
| Credit Profile and lender report | Company account | Shared |
| Trade & Export (contracts, buyers, export documents) | Company account | Shared |
| Weather (stations, fields, satellite readings) | Company account | Shared |
| Everything inside vendor workspaces | Company account | Shared |
| Data-sharing consent, province, district, household profile | Company account | Shared |
| Programme enrolment, extension visits, government and partner data | Company account | Shared |
| Animal Health | This browser only | Back up with Backup & Restore |
| Staff & Labour | This browser only | Back up |
| Procurement, Requisitions | This browser only | Back up |
| Processing recipes and batches | This browser only | Back up |
| Activity Log, Crop Rotation | This browser only | Back up |
| Device pages (Water, Aquaculture, Poultry Houses, Energy, Soil Depth, Automation, Cold Chain, Device Gateway, Farm Gateway, Smart Devices) and their alerts | This browser only | Back up |
| Smart Vision, Sustainability, Financial AI notes | This browser only | Back up |
| Notifications and the bell count | This browser only | They come from the device pages |
