| AgriFood Market, AgriSupply, AgriServices | **Listing boards**: a left list of adverts and a detail panel on the right | Sample adverts, plus any adverts you post. All are **saved on this device only** |
| Agro Dealers, Equipment Hire, Vet Services | **Provider directories**: cards for businesses, with a profile pop-up and a request form | Real registered providers from the server when it can be reached; otherwise sample providers |

> **Watch out:** There is **no shopping cart, no checkout and no payment** on any of these six pages in the farm workspace. There is also no page that lists your past orders or bookings, no "favourites" (saved items) button and no button to write a review. You contact the seller by phone, or you send a request that the provider answers by phone (see below). If you want to sell to other users, use a vendor workspace (see Part 6 — Vendor Workspaces: Basics, Vet Services, Equipment Hire, Transport and AgriServices and Part 7 — Vendor Workspaces: Agro Dealers, AgriSupply, AgriFood Sellers and Processors).

---

## AgriFood Market

### AgriFood Market

**What it is for.** A board of food adverts: poultry, livestock, fish, vegetables, fruit, grains, dairy, eggs and processed foods. You can browse what others have listed and post your own advert to sell produce.

**Before you start.** You need the **Marketplace** permission. Remember that the adverts are on **this device only**: people on other phones cannot see your advert.

**Step by step: browse and search**

1. Click **AgriFood Market** under **Marketplaces**.
2. On the **Browse** tab, type in **Search listings…** to look in titles and descriptions.
3. Use the category list. For this page it holds: **Poultry**, **Livestock**, **Fish & Seafood**, **Vegetables**, **Fruits**, **Grains & Cereals**, **Dairy**, **Eggs**, **Processed Foods**, **Other**.
4. Click an advert to open it on the right (on a phone it fills the screen; the X closes it).
5. To contact the seller, tap the phone number or the **Call Seller** button.

**What you will see on an advert**

| Item | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Title, category | What is for sale |
| Price and unit | For example 58 per bird. "negotiable" appears if the seller allows a bargain |
| Available | Quantity on offer |
| Description | Details from the seller |
| Seller | Name, phone number, location and the date posted |
| Tags | Short words such as organic, fresh, bulk |

The top of the list shows the number of adverts you can browse. Your own adverts never appear on the **Browse** tab; they appear on **My Listings**.

**Step by step: post your own advert**

1. Click **Sell Food** (top right of the list).
2. Fill in the window **Post New Listing** (below) and click **Post Listing**. The button stays grey until the title and price are filled in.
3. Open the **My Listings (n)** tab to see it.

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Title* | For example "Broiler Chickens – 500 birds ready" |
| Category | Pick from the list |
| Price* and Unit | For example 58 and "bird" (the unit starts as "kg") |
| Quantity Available | How many units you have |
| Location | For example "Lusaka" |
| Description | What you are selling |
| Your Name | Shown as the seller (blank becomes "Me") |
| Phone | The number buyers should call |
| Tags (comma-separated) | For example "organic, fresh, bulk" |
| Price negotiable | Tick if you accept offers |

**Managing your adverts.** Open one of your adverts and use the buttons under the price:

- **Edit** changes the title, price, unit, available quantity, description, location, phone and the negotiable box (not the category).
- **Mark as Sold** sets the advert to sold. It stays on **My Listings** with a green "Sold" label and the note "This listing is marked as sold and hidden from the public browse feed." Edit and Deactivate are then no longer offered.
- **Deactivate** hides the advert; **Reactivate** brings it back.
- **Delete** asks you to click **Confirm Delete**.

**Good to know**

- The six sample adverts (broilers from KwaZuma Farms, tilapia, tomatoes, maize, milk, honey) are examples with example phone numbers.
- Selling real produce through the platform is done from a vendor workspace (see Part 7).

**If something goes wrong**

- "No listings found." Clear the search words and set the category to "All Categories".
- "You have no listings yet." Post your first advert.

---

## AgriSupply

### AgriSupply

**What it is for.** The same listing board, for farm inputs: seeds, feed, fertiliser, chemicals, medicines and vaccines, day-old chicks, fingerlings, equipment, irrigation, storage, packaging.

**Step by step.** The steps are exactly those of **AgriFood Market** (search, category list, open an advert, **Call Seller**, **My Listings**). The posting button is **Sell Inputs** and the unit starts as "bag".

**Categories:** **Seeds**, **Animal Feed**, **Fertiliser**, **Chemicals & Pesticides**, **Medicines & Vaccines**, **Day-Old Chicks**, **Fingerlings**, **Equipment**, **Irrigation**, **Storage**, **Packaging**, **Other Inputs**.

**Sample adverts.** Seven examples (maize seed, broiler starter feed, NPK fertiliser, a solar pump, Newcastle vaccine, a drip kit, day-old broiler chicks). Prices are examples only.

> **Tip:** Always check the batch and expiry date of seed, vaccines and chemicals before you buy, and ask the seller for a receipt.

---

## AgriServices

### AgriServices

**What it is for.** The same listing board, for services you may need: vets, agronomists, transport, equipment hire, drone spraying, cold storage, irrigation work, accounting, training and labour.

**Step by step.** The steps are those of **AgriFood Market**. The posting button is **Offer Service** and the unit starts as "visit". Prices on a service advert are per visit, day, hour, trip, hectare, week, month or project, as the seller chooses.

**Categories:** **Veterinary**, **Agronomy**, **Transport**, **Equipment Hire**, **Aerial Services**, **Storage**, **Irrigation Services**, **Finance & Accounting**, **Training**, **Labour**, **Other Services**.

**Sample adverts.** Eight examples (a mobile vet, a crop scouting consultant, a 5-ton truck, tractor hire, drone spraying, cold-store space, irrigation installation, accounting and tax for farmers).

---

## Agro Dealers

### Agro Dealers

**What it is for.** A directory of shops that sell farm inputs (seed, fertiliser, chemicals, feed, equipment), with their prices, certificates and reviews, and a form to send an enquiry. The header says "Find & connect with verified providers".

**Before you start.** You need the **Marketplace** permission. For real providers your device must be able to reach the server. If it cannot, a yellow bar says "Showing sample data — connect to AgroNexus backend to see live provider profiles" and the cards are examples. While the list is loading a blue bar says "Loading provider directory…".

**Step by step: find a dealer**

1. Click **Agro Dealers** under **Marketplaces**. The **Browse All** tab opens.
2. Type in **Search providers...** (it looks in the name, tagline, description and tags).
3. Choose a district in **All Districts** (the list is built from the districts the providers cover).
4. Choose a category in **All Categories**. For Agro Dealers the categories are **Seeds**, **Fertiliser**, **Chemicals**, **Animal Feed**, **Equipment**, **Day-Old Chicks**, **Solar & Irrigation**, **Subsidised Inputs**.
5. Click a card to open the profile.

> **Note:** The category filter works on the services listed for each provider. If a live provider shows no services in the pop-up (see "Good to know"), choosing a category hides that provider.

**What a card shows.** Name, tagline, location with up to two coverage districts, a **Verified** badge (the business was checked) and a **Certified** badge (it lists certificates), a star rating with the number of reviews, three tags, the first two services with their price (for example "K650 per 50kg bag") and **View Profile**. Your own listing has a green **My Business** label.

**The profile pop-up.** It has a header with badges and rating, a contact strip (location, phone, email, website, opening hours, licence number) and four tabs:

| Tab | What it shows |
|---|---|
| Overview | Description, **Coverage Areas**, tags and, for dealers, the **Specialist crops / products** |
| Services (n) | Each service with its description, price ("POA" means price on application, ask the dealer), unit, category and **Available** or **Unavailable** |
| Reviews (n) | The average rating and each review: reviewer, how long ago, stars, comment and the service used |
| Certifications | Body, type, number and expiry date of each certificate |

The footer has **Call**, **Email**, **Website** (if there is one), **Book / Enquire** (not on your own listing) and, for verified providers, a **Video Call** button for a live consultation.

**Step by step: send an enquiry**

1. In the profile, click **Book / Enquire**. The window is called **Send Enquiry**.
2. Type what you need, with quantities, place and timing.
3. Click **Submit Request**. The window shows "Request submitted!" and "NAME will contact you on PHONE."

> **Watch out:** After you click **Submit Request** the screen always shows a friendly success message, even when the request could not be delivered (for example when you are offline, or when the provider is only a sample). The message then reads "Enquiry saved — provider will contact you shortly." but nothing may have been sent. A reference starting with **HB-** or **VA-** means the server really accepted the request. In every case, also **Call** the dealer. In this version the text of an enquiry may not be stored by the server, so phone to confirm the details.

**Register your own dealership.** Click **Register your dealership** (top right). Fill in **Business Name \***, **Tagline**, **Phone \***, **Email**, **Website**, **Location \***, **Districts (comma-separated)**, **Operating Hours**, **License Number**, **Tags (comma-separated)** and **Description**, then **Save Listing**. It appears on the **My Listings** tab with a green **My Business** label.

> **Watch out:** A listing registered here is kept **on this device only**. It is not sent to the server, other users cannot find it, and it has no services or reviews. The **Edit** button in the profile does nothing. To appear to other users, register your business as a vendor (see Part 7).

**Good to know**

- Sample dealers (six examples such as AgriInput Distributors, FeedMills Zambia, ZamPoultry Hatchery) are shown only when the server cannot be reached.
- For live providers, the Services, Reviews and Certifications tabs may show "No services listed yet.", "No reviews yet." or "No certifications uploaded." even when the dealer has entered them, because this farm screen shows only the headline details of a live provider. The rating and number of reviews on the card are still correct. Call the dealer for prices.
- Reviews come from approved reviews only. There is no button to write a review from the farm workspace.
- "No providers match your search." means your filters are too narrow. Clear them. If the server is reachable but no business of that type has registered yet, the list is also empty.

---

## Equipment Hire

### Equipment Hire

**What it is for.** A directory of businesses that hire out tractors, ploughs, harvesters, sprayers (including drones), irrigation pumps and pivots, with a booking form.

**Step by step: find equipment**

1. Click **Equipment Hire** under **Marketplaces**.
2. Search, choose a district and choose a category exactly as described under **Agro Dealers**. The categories here are **Land Prep**, **Planting**, **Spraying**, **Harvesting**, **Irrigation**, **Transport**, **Conservation**, **Mapping**.
3. Open a card to read the services and rates (for example "per ha", "per day", "per month"). The Overview tab may also show a **Mobile service** label (the owner comes to your farm) and the opening hours.

**Step by step: request a booking**

1. In the profile click **Book / Enquire**. The window is called **Book Equipment**.
2. Fill in the form and click **Submit Request**.

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Start Date*, End Date* | The days you need the machine (both start as today) |
| Hectares (optional) | The area to be worked |
| Delivery / Site Address | Your farm location or GPS point |
| Notes | Equipment type, soil conditions, special needs |

3. When the server accepts the request you see "Request submitted!" with a reference such as **HB-...**. The provider's record starts at the status **enquiry** and the provider contacts you on your phone to quote and confirm. The same warning applies as for Agro Dealers: if the message does not show a reference, phone the provider.

**Register your equipment business.** The button is **List your equipment**. The form and the "this device only" warning are the same as for Agro Dealers.

**Good to know**

- Five sample hire providers (FarmMech Services, AgroDrone Zambia, Harvest Solutions Zambia, IrriTech Hire, Musika Farm Equipment Hire) show when the server cannot be reached. Rates and licence numbers are examples.
- There is no deposit, payment or confirmation step on the farm side. Agree rates, the deposit and the dates with the provider by phone.
- To follow up a booking use the reference number and the provider's phone number, because the farm workspace has no list of your bookings.

---

## Vet Services

### Vet Services

**What it is for.** A directory of vets and animal health providers: mobile vets, clinics, aquaculture specialists, para-vets and remote consultation. You can request an appointment or start a video call.

**Step by step: find a vet**

1. Click **Vet Services** under **Marketplaces**.
2. Search, choose a district and choose a category as under **Agro Dealers**. The categories here are **Consultation**, **Vaccination**, **Diagnostics**, **Laboratory**, **Surgery**, **Biosecurity**, **Teleconsultation**, **Nutrition**, **Breeding**, **Water Quality**.
3. Open a card. The Overview tab may show the **coverage type**: **Mobile service** (comes to the farm), **Clinic-based** or **Teleconsultation**, and what the vet treats.

**Step by step: book an appointment**

1. In the profile click **Book / Enquire**. The window is called **Book Vet Service**.
2. Fill in the form and click **Submit Request**.

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Service Type* | General Consultation, Vaccination, Treatment / Procedure, Diagnosis / Lab, Deworming, Pregnancy Check, Teleconsultation (Remote), Other |
| Appointment Date* | The day you want (starts as today) |
| Species | For example "Broilers" or "Tilapia" |
| Animal Count | How many animals are affected |
| Symptoms / Reason | What you see |
| Additional Notes | Anything else (this box may not be stored by the server) |

3. On success you see "Request submitted!" and a reference such as **VA-...**. The request starts with the status **requested**, and the vet confirms by phone. If the message is "Appointment request saved — provider will confirm." with no reference, the request may not have reached the vet. Call to be sure.

**Video Call.** For vets and other verified providers the footer has a **Video Call** button. Click it to start a video consultation with the provider (the button shows "Starting…" while it connects).

> **Watch out:** If an animal is very sick, do not wait for an online request. Phone the vet or the nearest Department of Veterinary Services office at once. Newcastle disease and several other animal diseases must be reported to the Department.

**Register your practice.** The button is **Register your practice**; it works as described under **Agro Dealers** (this device only; use a vendor workspace to be found by farmers, see Part 6).

**Good to know**

- Five sample practices (AgriVet Zambia, Central Veterinary Clinic, Aqua Health Vets, Para-Vet Rural Services, TeleFarm Vet) show when the server cannot be reached. Fees are examples.
- Record the visit afterwards in **Animal Health** (see that chapter) and, to keep the cost, in Finance.

---

## Which page for which job

| Farm job | Page | Tab or button to use |
|---|---|---|
| Record a vaccination | Animal Health | **Vaccinations**, then **Add Vaccination** |
| Record a medicine given, with a withdrawal period | Animal Health | **Treatments**, then **Add Treatment** |
| Log a vet's visit and fee | Animal Health | **Vet Visits**, then **Add Vet Visit** |
| See which vaccinations are due or overdue | Animal Health | **Overview**, **Upcoming Vaccinations** |
| Know the date I can sell after a medicine | Animal Health | **Overview**, amber "Withdrawal period active" banner |
| Put the cost of a vaccination or treatment into Finance | Production (cycle events) | Record the event (see Part 3) |
| Keep a list of workers and their roles | Staff & Labour | **Directory**, **Add Employee** |
| Give a worker a real login and permissions | Team & Permissions | See Part 1 |
| Keep a list of suppliers | Procurement | **Suppliers**, **Add Supplier** |
| Raise a purchase order | Procurement | **Orders**, **Add Order** |
| Mark an order as delivered or paid | Procurement | **Orders**, **Mark Delivered** / **Mark Paid** |
| Ask the supervisor for feed or medicine | Requisitions | **New Request**, **Submit Now** |
| Approve or reject a stock request | Requisitions | **Pending Review**, **Approve** / **Reject** |
| Plan which crop follows which | Crop Rotation | **Overview** and **Planner** |
| Record what grew in a field last season | Crop Rotation | **Log Season** |
| See open alerts from device pages | Notifications | Status **Unresolved** |
| See today's feed and water plan | Smart Engine | **Per-Cycle Smart Recommendations (Today)** |
| See disease watch and the cycle calendar | AI Insights | **Disease Alerts**, **Crop Calendar** |
| See profit for one production cycle | Financial AI | **P&L** |
| See where my money goes (cost split) | Financial AI | **Costs** |
| Find how many units I must sell to break even | Financial AI | **Break-Even** |
| Keep track of a bank loan | Financial AI | **Loans**, **Add Loan**, **Record Payment** |
| Get a real forecast for my farm | Weather | **Stations**, then **Sync now**, then **Forecast** |
| Check crop greenness from satellite | Weather | **Fields & NDVI** |
| Record carbon, water, certificates and compliance tasks | Sustainability | **Carbon**, **Water**, **Certifications**, **Compliance** |
| Keep a list of my sensors | Smart Devices (or Device Gateway) | **All Devices**, **Add Device** |
| Log a tank level | Water Mgmt | **Tanks**, **Update Level** |
| Log pond feeding, water change or fish deaths | Aquaculture | **Feed**, **Log Water Change**, **Log Mortality** |
| Log eggs collected, poultry feed or bird deaths | Poultry Houses | **Collect Eggs**, **Feed Record**, **Mortality** |
| Log irrigation applied to a field zone | Soil Depth | **Log Irrigation** |
| Log grain silo temperature and moisture | Cold Chain | **Grain Silos**, **Log Reading** |
| Log a refrigerated truck's temperature | Cold Chain | **Refrigerated Trucks**, **Update Temp & Status** |
| Try out an IF/THEN rule | Automation | **Templates**, then **Run** |
| Look at energy use as an example | Energy | **Overview**, **Analytics** (sample data) |
| Buy seed, feed or fertiliser | Agro Dealers or AgriSupply | Open a provider or advert, **Call** |
| Hire a tractor or drone | Equipment Hire | **Book / Enquire** |
| Book a vet or ask for advice | Vet Services | **Book / Enquire** or **Video Call** |
| Sell produce on a local board | AgriFood Market | **Sell Food** (this device only) |
| Back up everything on this device | Backup & Restore | See Part 1 |

# Part 6 — Vendor Workspaces: Basics, Vet Services, Equipment Hire, Transport and AgriServices

This part explains the workspace you get when your business is **not** a farm or a cooperative. A "vendor" here means any business that sells a service or a product to farmers: a vet practice, an equipment-hire company, a haulage firm, an agro-dealer, a processor and so on.

Part 6 has five chapters.

- Chapter 1 explains everything that is the same for every vendor type: how the workspace is created, the screen layout, and the six tabs every vendor has (Clients, Invoices, Revenue, Marketplace Listing, Users & Permissions and Settings). Read this chapter first, whichever vendor type you are.
- Chapter 2 covers **Vet Services**.
- Chapter 3 covers **Equipment Hire**.
- Chapter 4 covers **Transport & Logistics**.
- Chapter 5 covers the **AgriServices Provider**.

Agro Dealers, AgriSupply, AgriFood Sellers and Processors have their own tabs and are described in Part 7 — Vendor Workspaces: Agro Dealers, AgriSupply, AgriFood Sellers and Processors. Those workspaces also use the common tabs described in Chapter 1, so Part 7 refers back here.

---

## Chapter 1 — Vendor workspace basics (applies to every vendor type)

### Who gets a vendor workspace

When you register a business account you choose a **business type**. The business types are:

| Business type | Workspace you get |
|---|---|
| Farmer / Farm, Cooperative | The farm workspace (see Parts 2 to 5), not a vendor workspace |
| Vet / Animal Health | **Vet Services** (Chapter 2) |
| Equipment Hire | **Equipment Hire** (Chapter 3) |
| Transport / Haulage | **Transport & Logistics** (Chapter 4) |
| AgriServices Provider | **AgriServices** (Chapter 5) |
| Agro Dealer | **Agro Dealer** (Part 7) |
| AgriSupply Provider | **AgriSupply** (Part 7) |
| AgriFood Seller | **AgriFood Seller** (Part 7) |
| Agro Processor | **Agro Processor** (Part 7) |

You are taken to your vendor workspace automatically after you finish the sign-up and set-up steps (see Part 1 — Getting Started and Your Account). You do not choose it from a menu. If you are signed in with a business type that is not one of these, the app falls back to the Agro Dealer workspace.

> **Note:** The business type is chosen when the account is created. If you picked the wrong one, ask your AGRINUXES contact to correct it, because the tabs you see depend on it.

### Your workspace is created for you on first sign-in

**What it is for.** Every vendor workspace is built around a *marketplace profile* (the app calls it a "provider profile"). This is the public face of your business: name, district, description, services, certificates. You do not have to create it by hand.

**What happens the first time you open the workspace.**

1. The page shows **Setting up your business workspace…** for a moment.
2. The app looks for a profile that belongs to your company. If none exists, it creates one from your business details.
3. The new profile takes your company name as its business name, your company district as its district, and lists your district as the only district you serve. Its marketplace category is chosen from your business type (see the table below). Its status is **Active**.
4. Your workspace opens on the **Overview** tab.

The marketplace category is picked like this:

| Your business type | Marketplace category created |
|---|---|
| Vet / Animal Health | Veterinary Services |
| Equipment Hire | Equipment Hire |
| Transport / Haulage | Transport & Logistics |
| AgriServices Provider | Agricultural Services |
| Agro Dealer, AgriSupply Provider | Agro Dealer / Input Supplier |
| Agro Processor | Agro Processing |
| AgriFood Seller | AgriFood Seller / Fresh Produce |

**Good to know**

- There is one profile per company. Everyone on your team who signs in sees the same profile and the same records.
- Your profile is created with status **Active** straight away, so it can appear in farmer searches before you have filled anything in. Complete it quickly (see "Marketplace Listing" below).
- Everything in a vendor workspace is saved to the company account, shared by all your team members, on any device. Nothing in these tabs is kept only in your browser.

**If something goes wrong**

| What you see | What it means |
|---|---|
| A red banner at the top of the page with a message | The workspace could not load its data. Check your internet connection and reload. |
| A red box saying "This tab hit an error: … Try another tab or reload." | One tab failed. Click a different tab, or reload the page. |

### The screen layout

**The sidebar (left).**

- At the top is a menu button (three lines). Click it to shrink the sidebar to icons only, or open it again.
- Next to it you see **AGRINUXES** and, under it, your vendor icon and business name.
- Below that is the list of tabs for your business type. The tab you are on is highlighted in green. Click any tab to open it.
- At the bottom is **Sign Out**.

**The top bar.** It shows where you are, for example "Vet Services / Appointments". On the right it shows your district. If you have not set a district it says **Set your district in Settings**.

**The work area.** The selected tab is shown here. Forms open as pop-up windows. When you save, delete or change something, a small green message (or red, if it failed) appears at the bottom right of the screen for a few seconds, for example "Saved." or "Business profile saved."

**Tab order.** Each vendor type has its own tabs first, then the same six common tabs in this order: **Clients**, **Invoices**, **Revenue**, **Marketplace Listing**, **Users & Permissions**, **Settings**.

> **Tip:** On a phone, use the menu button to collapse the sidebar so the tables have more room, and scroll tables sideways to see all columns.

### How statuses, colours and reference numbers work

Throughout the vendor tabs, a status appears as a small coloured label. The colour groups are:

| Colour | Typical statuses |
|---|---|
| Blue/sky | New, Requested, Enquiry, Received |
| Indigo | Quoted, Sent |
| Blue | Confirmed, Scheduled, Packed |
| Amber | Active, In progress, Processing, Out for delivery, Pending |
| Green | Completed, Delivered, Paid, Valid |
| Red | Cancelled, No show, Void, Rejected, Expired, Overdue, Suspended |
| Grey | Draft, Sold out, Inactive |

Each record gets an automatic reference number made of a prefix, the year and a running number:

| Record | Looks like |
|---|---|
| Invoice | INV-2026-0001 |
| Vet appointment | VA-2026-0001 |
| Equipment hire booking | HB-2026-0001 |
| Job (service or transport) | JOB-2026-0001 |
| Sales order (dealers, suppliers, sellers) | SO-2026-0001 |
| Processing lot | LOT-2026-0001 |

> **Note:** The running number counts every record of that kind on the whole platform for the year, not only yours. Your first invoice will therefore not be "0001". This is normal. Numbers can have gaps from your point of view.

### Overview (the first tab of every vendor type)

Each vendor type has its own Overview with its own four figures, described in the chapter for that type. They all share the same layout.

**What you see**

- **Title line:** the name of your workspace, then your business name, district, business type, and "verified" if the platform has verified you. A status label (usually **Active**) and, once you have reviews, a star rating with the number of reviews, for example "4.6 (12)".
- **Four figure cards** across the top (different for each vendor type).
- **Revenue received (last 6 months):** a bar chart with one bar per month, the current month on the right. On the right of the card title are two figures: the total received **year to date** (since 1 January) and received in the **last 30 days**. Under the chart you see how much came from each source, for example "Vet: ZMW 4,200.00". Until you record a payment, the chart says "No paid invoices, bookings or orders yet. Revenue appears here as payments are recorded."
- **Quick actions:** four buttons that jump to a tab. Below them are short reminders that appear only when they apply:
  - the number and total value of unpaid invoices;
  - follow-ups due within 7 days (vet);
  - machines due for service (equipment and transport);
  - listings low on stock (dealers and sellers; Part 7);
  - "Add a phone number in Settings so customers can reach you" if your phone is empty.
- A table of upcoming work (different for each vendor type).

> **Tip:** Make the Overview the first thing you open each morning. The reminders list is your to-do list.

---

### Clients

**What it is for.** A list of everybody you have dealt with, built automatically. You do not type clients in here.

**Before you start.** Nothing to set up. Clients appear as you record work.

**Where clients come from.** A client is added the first time you record any of these for them: a vet appointment, an equipment hire booking, a job, a sales order, or (for processors) a supplier of raw material. A client is either:

- a **platform client**, a farm or cooperative with an AGRINUXES account, whom you picked from the search box; or
- a **walk-in**, someone you typed in by name and phone. Walk-ins with the same name (capital letters ignored) are combined into one client.

**What you will see.** A heading "Clients" with the count, and a table:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Client | Name, and below it the phone number. If there is no phone it says "platform account" or "walk-in". |
| Type | The client's business type if they are a platform client, otherwise a dash. |
| District | The client's district if known. |
| Interactions | How many records you have for them, plus the kinds, for example "3 hire, vet". |
| Last | The date of the most recent record. |
| Total value | The sum of the agreed or total amounts on all their records. |
| Outstanding | The part of that total that is not yet marked paid (amber when above zero). |

The list is sorted with the most recent client first. If it is empty it says "No clients yet — they appear as you record bookings, appointments, jobs, orders or supplies."

**Good to know**

- You cannot edit or delete a client. To change a name, correct it on the record itself.
- *Outstanding* is worked out from the records (appointment, booking, job, order), not from invoices. A record counts as paid when its "paid" box is ticked, or when an invoice linked to it is marked paid.
- The figure labelled **Paying clients** on the Revenue tab is simply the number of clients in this list.

#### The client picker (used in every "new" form)

Every form that needs a client (new appointment, booking, job, order, invoice) uses the same picker. It has two parts side by side.

1. **Search platform client (farm / coop)…** — type at least two letters of the client's name. After a short pause, up to ten matching farm and cooperative accounts are listed with their district and business type. Click one to choose it. The name then appears locked in the box. Click **clear** to undo.
2. **or walk-in name \*** and **Phone** — for a customer without an account, type the name (required) and a phone number (optional). When a platform client is chosen, the walk-in name box is disabled and says "Platform client selected". You can still type a phone number.

**If something goes wrong**

| Message | What it means |
|---|---|
| Give a client organisation or a client name | You left both the search and the walk-in name empty. |
| Client organisation not found | The platform client you picked no longer exists. Search again. |

> **Note:** The search only finds farm and cooperative accounts, not other vendors, and never your own company.

---

### Invoices

**What it is for.** To bill a client and track who has paid. Invoices work the same in every vendor type. You can write one from scratch or create it straight from an appointment, hire booking or job.

**Before you start.** Have the client (platform or walk-in) and the lines to bill. Decide what to write under **Notes / payment details**, for example your Mobile Money number or bank details, because the client sees these on the invoice.

**What you will see on the Invoices tab**

Four cards at the top:

| Card | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Invoices | The total number of invoices. |
| Awaiting payment | How many are **Sent** or **Overdue**, and their total value. |
| Overdue | How many are overdue. |
| Paid | How many are paid, and their total value. |

Below is the **Invoices** table with a **+ New invoice** button:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Ref | The invoice number. Click it to open a printable view. |
| Client | Platform client name, or the walk-in name, or "Walk-in". |
| Issued / Due | Issue date and due date. |
| For | The reference of the appointment, booking, job or order it was created from, if any. |
| Total | Amount including tax, in ZMW. |
| Status | Draft, Sent, Paid, Overdue or Void. |

The last column holds action buttons that depend on the status (see below).

**Step by step: create an invoice from scratch**

1. Open **Invoices**.
2. Click **+ New invoice**. A window **New invoice** opens.
3. Choose the client with the client picker (see above).
4. Fill in the first line: **Description**, **Qty** (quantity, starts at 1) and **Unit price**.
5. To add more lines, click **+ Add line**. To remove a line, click the **×** at its right. Lines with an empty description are ignored.
6. In **Tax / VAT amount** type the tax as an *amount in kwacha*, not a percentage. It starts at 0.
7. Choose a **Due date**. If you leave it empty, the due date is set to 14 days after the issue date.
8. In **Notes / payment details** write how the client should pay.
9. Watch the line at the bottom: **Subtotal** (sum of quantity × unit price) and **Total** (subtotal plus tax).
10. Click **Save draft**. The invoice is saved with status **Draft** and a number such as INV-2026-0014. The issue date is today.

**Step by step: create an invoice from a record**

1. Open the appointment (Vet), booking (Equipment Hire) or job (Transport, AgriServices) tab.
2. On a record that is **Completed** and not yet paid, click **Invoice**.
3. A window named "Invoice for [reference]" opens. The client and lines are taken from the record. For example a hire booking becomes one line "Hire of [machine] [start date] → [end date]" priced at the agreed amount (or the quoted amount if there is no agreed amount). Add lines in the window only if you want to *replace* the automatic ones.
4. Add tax, due date and notes if you wish, then click **Save draft**.

**Step by step: send and collect payment**

1. A **Draft** invoice shows three buttons: **Send**, **Edit** and **Delete**.
2. Click **Send** when you hand the invoice to the client. The status changes to **Sent** and the message "Invoice INV-… marked sent." appears. In this version, **Send** only changes the status; it does not email or text the client. Click the invoice number and use **Print** (it uses your browser's print dialog, so you can also save a PDF) or read the details out.
3. A **Sent** or **Overdue** invoice shows **Mark paid** and **Void**.
4. When money arrives, click **Mark paid**. A list **Payment method** appears with **Cash**, **Mobile Money**, **Bank**, **Card** and **Voucher**. Choose one and click **Confirm paid** (or **Cancel**).
5. The invoice becomes **Paid** with today's date as the payment date. If it was linked to an appointment, booking, job or order, that record is also marked paid.
6. Use **Void** to cancel an invoice that was issued by mistake. Voiding cannot be undone.

**Statuses**

| Status | What it means | What you can do |
|---|---|---|
| Draft | Written but not given to the client. | Send, Edit, Delete |
| Sent | Given to the client, waiting for payment. | Mark paid, Void |
| Overdue | Was Sent and the due date has passed. The app sets this itself when you open the Invoices tab. | Mark paid, Void |
| Paid | Money received. | Nothing; the invoice is locked. |
| Void | Cancelled. | Nothing; the invoice is locked. |

**The invoice view.** Clicking a number opens the invoice: your business name, "Invoice to" with the client name and phone, issue and due dates, status, the reference of the linked record, the lines (description, quantity, unit price, line total), subtotal, tax, total, the payment date and method if paid, and your notes. Buttons: **Print** and **Close**.

**Good to know**

- A draft has to be **Sent** before it can be marked paid; there is no shortcut from Draft to Paid.
- Only drafts can be deleted. After that, void.
- Paid and void invoices cannot be edited.
- The currency is ZMW.
- After you create an invoice from a completed record, the **Invoice** button on that record stays visible until the record is marked paid. Check the Invoices tab before you click it a second time, or you will create a duplicate.
- A vet invoice adds each medication as an extra line at price 0, so the client can see what was used. The money is on the consultation line.
- If a record has no amount (no agreed, quoted, total or fee amount), an invoice made from it has a line priced at 0. Edit the draft and enter the real price.

**If something goes wrong**

| Message | What it means |
|---|---|
| An invoice needs at least one line | You saved with no description filled in. |
| Item quantity and unit price must be numbers | A line has text where a number should be. |
| Item values cannot be negative | A quantity or price is below zero. |
| Paid or void invoices cannot be edited | The invoice is locked. Create a new one. |
| Only draft invoices can be deleted — void it instead | Use **Void** on a sent invoice. |
| Linked record not found | The appointment, booking or job was removed. |

---

### Revenue

**What it is for.** To see the money you have actually received. It counts cash in, not work still waiting to be paid.

**What you will see**

Four cards:

| Card | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Last 30 days | Money received in the last 30 days. |
| Year to date | Money received since 1 January. |
| Outstanding | Total of invoices that are Sent or Overdue, and how many there are. |
| Paying clients | The number of clients in your Clients list. |

Below are:

- **Monthly revenue received:** a bar chart for the last six months, with a list of the six monthly totals under it.
- **By source:** the total for each kind of income, biggest first. Sources are Invoice (unlinked), Vet, Hire, Job and Order.
- **Recent payments:** a table of your 20 most recent paid invoices with date, invoice number, client, payment method and amount.

**How the revenue figure is worked out.** The app adds up four kinds of paid items:

1. Every **Paid invoice**, counted on the day it was paid.
2. Every vet appointment ticked **Paid in full** that has a total amount and is *not* already covered by a paid invoice, counted on the appointment date.
3. Every hire booking ticked **Paid in full** that has an agreed amount and is not covered by a paid invoice, counted on the booking's end date.
4. Every job ticked **Paid** that has an agreed amount, and every paid sales order, that is not covered by a paid invoice. Jobs are counted on the completion date, orders on the delivery date.

A paid invoice always wins over the record it was made from, so nothing is counted twice.

> **Watch out:** A "Paid" tick on its own does not add to Revenue unless the record also has an amount (agreed amount for bookings and jobs, total charge for appointments). Always fill in the money amount.

> **Note:** The Revenue tab does not include income from outside AGRINUXES records, and it does not subtract expenses. The Recent payments table lists paid invoices only, not paid records that were never invoiced.

---

### Marketplace Listing (your public profile)

**What it is for.** To control what farmers and buyers see about your business. The tab is called **Marketplace Listing**, and the text on it says everything below the first card is public.

**What you will see, from top to bottom**

1. **Public marketplace profile** card, a preview:
   - business name, tagline (or "No tagline yet — add one in Settings");
   - marketplace category, district, the districts you serve, and, if you tick them in Settings, "comes to the farm" and "emergency calls";
   - your description;
   - on the right: your status, your verification state, your rating, number of reviews and number of bookings.
2. A services card (**Services & fees**, **Other services**, **Routes & rates**, **Services offered** or **Services & rates**, depending on your type). See "Services and prices" below.
3. **Certifications & licences.**
4. **Customer reviews.**

**Verification.** A tick "Verified by AGRINUXES" appears only after a platform administrator has checked your certificates. Until then the card says "Not yet verified — verification is done by platform admins after checking certifications." You cannot verify yourself. A profile that is not verified is still listed and can still receive bookings.

**To change the preview text** (tagline, description, phone, district and so on) use the **Settings** tab. This tab only shows it.

> **Tip:** Treat this tab as your shop window. Before you tell farmers about AGRINUXES, open it and ask: "If I were a farmer in my district, would I know what this business does, where, and what it costs?"

#### Services and prices

**What it is for.** The list of services (or products) you offer with prices. In the Vet and AgriServices workspaces the same list also has its own tab (**Services Catalog**), and the same entries appear in both places.

**Step by step**

1. Click **+ Add** at the top right of the services card.
2. Fill in the window **Add service / product**:

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Name * | Required. For example "Farm visit" or "Ploughing". |
| Category | Free text, for example "Vaccination". |
| Pricing model | How the price works. Choices below. A new service starts as **Negotiable / quote**. |
| Price | The amount in ZMW. |
| Price max (range) | The top of a range, if the price varies, for example 400 to 600. |
| Unit label (e.g. 50 kg bag) | Words that explain the unit, for example "per 1,000 birds" or "per trip". |
| Lead time (days) | How many days notice you need. Empty means "Immediate". |
| Available | Tick when you can currently provide it. |
| Description | A short explanation farmers will read. |

3. Click **Save**. The message "Saved." appears.

The **Pricing model** choices are:

| Choice | Use it when |
|---|---|
| Fixed price | One price for the whole service. |
| Per unit | Price per item or bag; use the unit label to say which. |
| Per hectare | Priced per ha (ploughing, spraying). |
| Per head / animal | Priced per animal (vaccination). |
| Per hour | Priced per hour of work. |
| Per day | Priced per day. |
| Per km | Priced per kilometre (transport). |
| Negotiable / quote | You give a quote for each job. |
| Free | No charge. |

**What the table shows:** service name and description, category, pricing (for example "ZMW 350.00 – 500.00 Per head / animal · per 1,000 birds"), lead time, a tick under **Available**, and **Edit** and **Delete** buttons. **Delete** asks "Delete [name]? This cannot be undone."

**Good to know**

- The currency is ZMW and cannot be changed in the form.
- There is no "per tonne" choice. For tonnes, choose **Per unit** and write "tonne" in the unit label, or choose **Fixed price** and write "per trip".
- When you start a job, you can pick a service from this list to fill in the job title and quoted price (AgriServices; see Chapter 5).

#### Certifications and licences

**What it is for.** To show the papers that prove you are allowed to do your work, for example SCCI, ZABS, VRAZ, ZEMA, or the Veterinary Board registration. Buyers and programmes look for these.

**Step by step**

1. Click **+ Add** in the **Certifications & licences** card.
2. Fill in the window **Certification**:

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Certificate name * | For example "Veterinary Practice Licence". |
| Issuing body | The organisation that issued it. |
| Certificate number | The number on the paper. |
| Issued / Expires | Dates. |
| Document link | A web link to a scan or photo, if you have one stored online. |

3. Click **Save**.

**What the table shows:** certificate, issuing body, number, expiry date, a **Status** label (Valid, Expired or Pending), **Verified** (shows "platform" when an administrator has checked it) and **Edit / Delete**.

**Good to know**

- If you enter an expiry date that has already passed, the app sets the status to **Expired** when you save.
- The form has no status box. A new certificate starts as **Valid**.
- You cannot upload a file in this form. The *Document link* is a web address only.
- Only a platform administrator can mark a certificate "verified".

#### Customer reviews

This card lists reviews farmers have written about you: stars, title, text, the farm's name, the date, and either **published** or **awaiting moderation**. Reviews are published only after the platform moderates them. Until then they are visible to you but not to buyers. With no reviews it says "No reviews yet. Customers can review you after a booking or appointment; reviews are published after platform moderation." Each farm can leave one review per provider, and a new review from the same farm replaces its earlier one.

---

### Users & Permissions

**What it is for.** To give your staff their own logins and decide what each can do. This is the same screen used in the farm workspace (see Part 1 — Getting Started and Your Account, and Part 4 — Running the Farm Business for the full description). This section covers what is particular to vendors.

**Who sees it.** The tab only appears for people whose permissions include "Team & Permissions". A company director (the account owner) and any administrator always see it. Other people may not see the tab at all.

**What you will see.** A page **Team & Permissions** with three small tabs:

- **Members** — add people, change a person's role and branch, make or remove an administrator, deactivate or reactivate, and open a person's permission table (shield button). Choose **Add member**, then enter full name, phone (used to log in if there is no email), optional email, role, branch, and optionally a password. If you leave the password empty a temporary password is shown once; copy it and send it to the person privately.
- **Branches** — add branches (for example a depot or a second clinic) and assign people to them.
- **Audit log** — administrators only; a list of access changes.

Only administrators can add or change people. Everyone else sees the page read-only, with the note "read-only (you are not an administrator)".

> **Watch out:** The role list on this page is the standard business role list (Director / Owner, Production Manager, Finance Manager, Sales Manager, Supervisor, Farmhand, Veterinary Officer and Driver). These roles were designed for farms. The vendor tabs described in this part are **not** switched on and off by those module permissions: apart from the Users & Permissions tab itself, every person who can sign in to your company account sees every vendor tab and can create, change and delete records in it. Add only people you trust with your invoices and client list.

> **Note:** The people you add here are *logins*. They are different from the *Team* list in Settings, which is a list of staff (vets, drivers, operators) shown on your profile and used to assign work. See the next section.

---

### Settings

The Settings tab has three blocks, in this order.

#### 1. Share with government & partners

This block appears first when your company is a business (farm-type) account. It has a switch and two boxes.

- **Share with government & partners** — when switched on, your organisation's counts are included in anonymous district and national dashboards used by the Ministry of Agriculture, extension services and development partners. Your name is never shown. You also receive district advisories.
- **Province** and **District** with a **Save** button — these place your company on district dashboards.
- **Household profile** — appears if you are the director; used by programmes that report by sex, age and household size.

Only a **director** can change the switch, the province and district, or the household profile. Others see them greyed out and the hint "Only a director can change this".

> **Note:** The **District** in this block belongs to your *company*. The **District** in the Business profile below belongs to your *marketplace profile*. They are two separate fields. Set both.

#### 2. Business profile

This is the form that feeds your public profile. Fill it in fully.

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Business name * | Required. Shown everywhere. |
| Marketplace category | The type of provider farmers will look for. The choices are: Agro Dealer / Input Supplier, Equipment Hire, Veterinary Services, Agro Processing, Transport & Logistics, Agricultural Finance, Crop / Livestock Insurance, Extension & Advisory Services, Cold Storage, Certification & Inspection, AgriFood Seller / Fresh Produce, Agricultural Services. |
| Tagline | One line customers see first. |
| Description | A short paragraph about what you do. |
| Phone / WhatsApp | How customers reach you. Add at least a phone. |
| Email / Website | Optional. |
| District / Town / Address | Where you are based. |
| Districts served (comma-separated) | For example "Chongwe, Kafue, Chilanga". Farmers searching those districts can find you. |
| Specialties (comma-separated) | For example "Poultry, dairy, maize seed". Used in search. |
| Service hours | For example "Mon-Fri 08:00-17:00". |
| Year established | A year. |
| Business registration no. / TPIN / tax id | Optional. |
| We travel to the farm | Tick if you go to the customer. |
| Emergency call-outs available | Tick if you take emergency calls. |

Click **Save profile**. The message "Business profile saved." appears, and the top bar updates.

**Good to know**

- The form has no place for a logo, photos or map position. Those cannot be added from this screen.
- Separate several districts or specialties with commas.
- Changing the **Marketplace category** changes where farmers find you, so change it only if the current one is wrong.

#### 3. Team (staff list)

**What it is for.** A list of the people who do the work: vet officers, drivers, operators, technicians. People on this list can be chosen in drop-down lists, such as the **Vet officer** on an appointment or **Assigned to** on a job. They do not get a login from this list.

**Step by step**

1. Click **+ Add person**.
2. Fill in the window **Add team member**:

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Full name * | Required. |
| Role | Owner / Director, Branch Manager, Veterinary Officer, Agronomist, Sales Representative, Technician, Driver / Operator, or Other. A new person starts as Technician. |
| Phone / Email | Contact details. |
| Licence / registration no. | Their professional licence, for example a veterinary registration number. |
| Active | Tick while they work for you. Only active people appear in the "Assigned to" list on jobs and in the "Vet officer" list on new appointments. |
| Bio | A short description. |

3. Click **Save**.

The table shows name, role, phone, licence, active tick, and **Edit** and **Remove** buttons. **Remove** asks you to confirm.

> **Tip:** Untick **Active** instead of removing a person who leaves. Their name stays on old records.

---

### First week checklist (all vendors)

1. Open the workspace and let it finish setting up.
2. Go to **Settings**. Fill in the whole **Business profile**, especially phone, WhatsApp, district, districts served, description and service hours. Click **Save profile**.
3. Set the province and district in the **Share with government & partners** block, and decide about the switch.
4. In **Settings** add your staff under **Team** (vets, drivers, operators).
5. Open **Marketplace Listing** and add your services with prices.
6. Add your certificates under **Certifications & licences**.
7. Add your people as logins in **Users & Permissions** if others will use the app.
8. Do one trial invoice: create it, send it, mark it paid, check **Revenue**, then void or leave it, depending on your records. (A paid invoice is permanent, so use a small real job instead of a made-up one.)

### Daily routine (all vendors)

1. Open **Overview** and read the reminders.
2. Handle new requests in your main tab (appointments, bookings or jobs).
3. Record work done and payments received as soon as they happen.
4. Open **Invoices** and chase anything **Overdue**.
5. At the end of the week, look at **Revenue** and **Clients** (the *Outstanding* column).

---

## Chapter 2 — Vet Services

The Vet Services workspace is for vet practices, mobile vets and para-vets. Its tabs, in order, are:

| Tab | What it does |
|---|---|
| **Overview** | Today's picture: upcoming appointments, follow-ups, clients, money received. |
| **Appointments** | Every booking and walk-in visit; where you record the clinical outcome. |
| **Patient Records** | One card per client and animal species, built from your appointments. |
| **Prescriptions** | A register of every medication you recorded. |
| **Services Catalog** | Your services and fees (called "Services & fees"). |
| **Clients, Invoices, Revenue, Marketplace Listing, Users & Permissions, Settings** | The common tabs from Chapter 1. |

Before you begin, finish the **First week checklist** in Chapter 1. In particular add your vet officers under **Settings > Team** with role **Veterinary Officer** and their licence number, so they can be chosen on appointments.

### Overview (Vet)

**What you will see**

| Card | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Upcoming appointments | Appointments dated today or later whose status is **Requested** or **Confirmed**. Under it: how many of the next appointments are today. |
| Follow-ups due (7 d) | Appointments (not cancelled) that have a follow-up date within the next 7 days. Follow-ups already past their date are counted too. |
| Clients | The number of clients on your Clients list. |
| Received (30 d) | Money received in the last 30 days, and how many invoices are unpaid. |

Under the cards are the revenue chart and **Quick actions** (**New appointment**, **Patient records**, **Prescriptions**, **Invoices**), then **Next appointments**: up to eight appointments from today onward that are not completed, cancelled or no-show, with date and time, client, type, animals (for example "Cattle × 12"), vet officer and status. If there are none it says "Nothing scheduled. Farmers book you from the marketplace; walk-ins go in under Appointments."

### Appointments

**What it is for.** The working diary of the practice. Every farmer booking from the marketplace and every walk-in or phone visit you enter lives here, from first request to payment.

**Before you start.** Add your vet officers in Settings. Decide your standard consultation fee, or list it in the Services Catalog.

**Appointment statuses**

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Requested | A farmer asked for a visit through the marketplace, or you entered it as a request. Waiting for you to confirm. |
| Confirmed | You agreed the date. |
| In progress | The vet is with the animals now. |
| Completed | The visit is finished and the outcome is recorded. |
| No show | The farmer did not turn up. |
| Cancelled | The visit was cancelled. |

**The table.** The **Appointments** card has a status filter (**All statuses** or one status) and a **+ Appointment** button. Columns:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Ref / date | Date and time (blue), the reference such as VA-2026-0007, and "via marketplace" or "walk-in". |
| Client | Name, then phone or the client's district. |
| Type & animals | The visit type and the species and number of animals. |
| Reason | The reason or symptoms; if you have recorded one, "Dx:" and your diagnosis in teal. |
| Officer | The vet officer, or a dash. |
| Fee | The total charge if set, otherwise the consultation fee. A green "paid" appears when paid. |
| Status | The status label. |

The last column has buttons that depend on the status:

| Button | Shown when | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| **Confirm** | Status is Requested | Sets the status to Confirmed. |
| **Start** | Requested or Confirmed | Sets the status to In progress. |
| **Record outcome** / **Edit record** | Any status except Cancelled and No show (**Edit record** once it is Completed) | Opens the clinical record window. |
| **Invoice** | Completed and not yet paid | Opens an invoice made from the appointment. |
| **Cancel** | Requested or Confirmed | Sets the status to Cancelled. |

> **Note:** "via marketplace" is shown for any appointment linked to a platform account. That includes appointments you entered yourself after picking a platform client in the client picker. "walk-in" means a client typed in by name.

#### Types of visit

The **Type** choices are: General consultation, Vaccination, Treatment / procedure, Diagnosis / lab, Deworming, Pregnancy check, Pre-slaughter certification, Teleconsultation and Other.

The **Species** choices (when you book) are: Cattle, Goats, Sheep, Pigs, Broilers, Layers, Village chickens, Tilapia, Dogs and Other.

### Step by step: receive and manage a farmer's booking

1. A farmer books you from the marketplace (see "How farmers find and book you" at the end of this chapter). The appointment appears in **Appointments** with status **Requested** and the words "via marketplace". It also counts in **Upcoming appointments** on the Overview.
2. Read the **Reason** column. The farmer may have written symptoms. Phone the farmer if you need more, using the phone number in the client column (or in their account).
3. Click **Confirm** once you agree the date.
4. If you want a particular officer on it, click **Record outcome** at any time and choose the **Vet officer** (you can do this before the visit and save with the status **Confirmed**).
5. On the day, click **Start** when you begin. The status becomes **In progress**.
6. When the visit is over, click **Record outcome** and fill in the clinical record (see below).
7. Click **Invoice** if you bill by invoice, or tick **Paid in full** in the outcome window if the farmer paid on the spot.

If the farmer does not come, open **Record outcome**, set **Status** to **No show** and save. To cancel before the visit use **Cancel**.

> **Note:** Once an appointment is Cancelled or No show there are no buttons left on it. Create a new appointment if the farmer wants another date.

### Step by step: enter a walk-in or phone appointment

1. Click **+ Appointment**. The window **New appointment** opens.
2. Choose the client in the client picker: search for a platform client, or type a walk-in name and phone.
3. Fill in the form:

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Type | The kind of visit (list above). Starts as General consultation. |
| Vet officer | Choose from your active staff, or leave **Unassigned**. |
| Date * | The visit date (required). Starts as today. |
| Time | The time. Starts at 09:00. Clear it for "no set time". |
| Species | Starts as Cattle. |
| Number of animals | The count, 0 or more. |
| Reason / symptoms | What is wrong or what the visit is for. |
| Consultation fee (ZMW) | The fee you will charge. It becomes the total if you do not enter a different total later. |
| Status | **Requested**, **Confirmed** or **In progress**. Starts as **Confirmed**. |

4. Click **Book**. The message "Appointment booked." appears.

**If something goes wrong:** "Give a client organisation or a client name" means you did not choose a client or type a walk-in name. "Invalid appointment type" or "Vet officer not found" mean the form was out of date; close it and try again.

### Step by step: record a consultation or treatment (the clinical record)

1. In **Appointments**, click **Record outcome** on the visit. The window is titled with the reference, the client and the species.
2. Fill in:

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Status | **Confirmed**, **In progress**, **Completed**, **No show** or **Cancelled**. The window opens on **Completed**; change it if the visit is not finished. |
| Vet officer | Who did the work. All staff are listed here. |
| Species / Animals seen | Correct them if the numbers on the day were different. |
| Diagnosis | Your finding, for example "Newcastle disease (suspected)" or "Mastitis (subclinical)". |
| Treatment given | What you did. |
| Prescription / medications | See below. |
| Follow-up / next vaccination date | The date of the next visit or booster. |
| Total charge (ZMW) | The full amount for this visit. It starts from the consultation fee. |
| Notes | Advice to the farmer, biosecurity, withdrawal reminders. |
| Paid in full | Tick if the farmer has paid. |

3. Click **Save record**. The message "Clinical record saved." appears.

**Good to know**

- You can open **Edit record** on a completed appointment as often as you like, to add or correct things.
- If you leave **Total charge** empty and the appointment has a consultation fee, the total is set to the fee.
- A follow-up date makes the visit appear in **Follow-ups due (7 d)** on the Overview when it is within seven days.

### Writing a prescription

**What it is for.** To record every drug or vaccine given or prescribed, in the same window as the diagnosis. The app keeps these records as a register (the **Prescriptions** tab) which is the kind of record a VRAZ inspection asks for.

**Step by step**

1. In the outcome window, find **Prescription / medications**. The column titles are **Drug / vaccine**, **Dose**, **Route**, **Days** and **Withdrawal (d)**.
2. Click **+ Add medication** to add a row.
3. Fill in the row:

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Drug / vaccine | For example "Oxytetracycline 20%". A row with no name is thrown away when you save. |
| Dose | For example "1 ml/10 kg". |
| Route | Choose from IM (injection into muscle), SC (under the skin), IV (into a vein), Oral, Topical (on the skin), In water, In feed. |
| Days | How many days the course lasts. |
| Withdrawal (d) | The number of days after the last dose before the animal's milk, eggs or meat may be sold or eaten. |

4. Click the **×** to remove a row. Click **Save record** when done.

> **Watch out:** Always fill in **Withdrawal (d)** for drugs that have a withdrawal period. The farmer relies on it before selling meat, milk or eggs. The app does not calculate it for you and does not send reminders.

> **Note:** AGRINUXES records what you prescribed or gave. It does not keep a pharmacy stock for vets, so nothing is taken out of any inventory when you record a medication. If you sell medicines as products, use an Agro Dealer workspace or keep your own stock register.

### Prescriptions (register)

**What it is for.** A read-only list of every medication recorded on any appointment. It cannot be edited here; correct an item by opening **Edit record** on its appointment.

**What you will see.** A card titled **Prescription register** with the number of lines, and a table:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Date | The appointment date. |
| Client | The farm or person. |
| Animals | Species and number. |
| Drug / vaccine | The medicine name. |
| Dose, Route, Days | As entered. |
| Withdrawal | The withdrawal days, for example "7 d". |
| Prescribed by | The vet officer on the appointment. |

With nothing recorded it says "No prescriptions yet. Add medications when you record an appointment outcome." The list has no search box and no export button in this version.

### Patient Records

**What it is for.** To see, per client and per kind of animal, how many animals you look after, how often you have visited, what conditions you found and what is owed. It is built by the app from your appointments; you do not add records here.

**How records are grouped.** One card is made for each **client + species** pair, for example "Mwamba Farm — Cattle". Cancelled appointments are left out. A platform client and a walk-in with the same name are different cards.

**What you will see**

Four cards at the top:

| Card | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Herds / flocks on record | The number of cards. |
| Animals under care | The total of the animal counts on all cards. |
| Follow-ups scheduled | Cards that have a future follow-up date. |
| Outstanding fees | The total of unpaid visit charges. |

Below is **Patient records** with a search box ("Search client, species, condition"). Each card shows:

- the client name, an icon for the species, the species and the phone number;
- the number of animals in large type ("head");
- the number of visits and the date of the last visit, with "follow-up [date]" in amber if one is coming;
- the diagnoses you have recorded, as small tags;
- the medications used, after a pill icon;
- "Owes ZMW …" in red if the client has unpaid completed charges.

With no data it says "Patient records build up automatically from appointments: one card per client and species."

**Good to know**

- The animal count on a card is the *largest* number you recorded on a single visit, not a running total.
- Owed amounts count appointments that have a total charge and are not ticked paid.
- There are no records of individual animals (tag numbers, names) in this version; the record is per herd, flock or group.
- You cannot click a card to open it. To change the details, open the appointment.

### Services Catalog (Vet)

This tab is the **Services & fees** list described in Chapter 1 under "Services and prices". The hint on the screen says: "Consultations, vaccinations, farm visits, lab work — with pricing per head, per visit or per hour."

Suggested entries for a practice in Chongwe:

| Name | Pricing model | Price | Unit label |
|---|---|---|---|
| Farm visit and consultation | Fixed price | 350 | per visit |
| Newcastle vaccination | Per head / animal | 2 | per bird |
| Cattle dipping advice | Per hour | 150 | per hour |
| Post-mortem | Fixed price | 450 | per case |

> **Example:** These figures are only an illustration of how to fill the form. Use your own prices.

### Invoicing a visit

1. Complete the clinical record and make sure **Total charge** is correct.
2. In **Appointments** click **Invoice** on the completed appointment. (The button is shown while the appointment is Completed and not ticked Paid.)
3. In the window "Invoice for VA-…", check the lines. The first line is the visit type, species, number of animals and date, priced at the total charge (or the consultation fee). Each medication is added as a line at price 0.
4. Add tax and a due date if needed and click **Save draft**.
5. Go to **Invoices**, click **Send**, and later **Mark paid** with the payment method. The appointment is then marked **Paid** automatically.

If the farmer pays at the clinic, you may skip the invoice and just tick **Paid in full** in the outcome window. Remember that the revenue figure needs a total charge on the appointment.

### What the farmer sees when booking a vet

In the farm workspace, under the Markets section of the left menu, there is a **Vet Services** page. It shows a list of practices as cards: name, tagline, location, the districts served, a **Verified** badge (if the platform has verified you), the star rating and a few tags. The farmer can search by name or description, and filter by district.

When the farmer opens your card they see tabs **Overview** (description, coverage areas and tags), **Services**, **Reviews** and **Certifications**, with your phone, email, website and hours at the top. They click **Book / Enquire** and fill in:

| Field the farmer sees | Notes |
|---|---|
| Service Type * | General Consultation, Vaccination, Treatment / Procedure, Diagnosis / Lab, Deworming, Pregnancy Check, Teleconsultation (Remote), Other. (Pre-slaughter certification is not offered here.) |
| Appointment Date * | A date. There is no time field. |
| Species / Animal Count | Free text and a number. |
| Symptoms / Reason | What is wrong. This arrives in your **Reason** column. |
| Additional Notes | Not stored on the appointment in this version, so do not rely on it. |

After sending, the farmer sees "Request submitted!" and a reference number starting with VA-, and the message that your business will contact them on your phone number. **That is why your phone number must be filled in.**

> **Watch out:** In this version the farmer's view of a live practice shows your description, districts, tags, contact details, hours and rating. The **Services**, **Reviews** and **Certifications** tabs on the farmer's card may read "No services listed yet." or "No reviews yet." even when you have filled in your own lists. Because of this, put your main prices and services in your **Description** and **Tagline** too, and make sure your phone number is correct so farmers can call.

### First week checklist (Vet)

1. Complete the common checklist in Chapter 1 (profile, phone, districts, hours).
2. Tick **We travel to the farm** if you do farm visits, and **Emergency call-outs available** if you do emergencies.
3. Add every vet officer and para-vet under **Settings > Team** with licence numbers.
4. Add 5 to 10 services with fees in **Services Catalog**.
5. Add your Veterinary Board registration and any other certificates under **Marketplace Listing**.
6. Enter three real appointments (**+ Appointment**) and record the outcome of each, including one medication with a withdrawal period.
7. Check **Patient Records** and **Prescriptions** to see them appear.
8. Invoice one completed visit and mark it paid. Look at **Revenue**.
9. Ask a friendly farmer to open **Vet Services** in the farm workspace and book you, so you see the "via marketplace" flow once.

### Daily routine (Vet)

1. **Overview**: check **Upcoming appointments** and **Follow-ups due (7 d)**.
2. **Appointments**: filter **Requested**; **Confirm** or phone each one.
3. During the day: **Start** and **Record outcome** for each visit, with diagnosis, medications and total charge.
4. After the visit: **Invoice** or tick **Paid in full**.
5. Evening: look at **Invoices** for overdue items, and at **Patient Records** for clients that owe you.

### How farmers find and book you (Vet)

1. **Fill in** (all in Settings and Marketplace Listing): business name, tagline, description, phone and WhatsApp, district, districts served, specialties (for example "Poultry, cattle, tilapia"), service hours, the travel and emergency boxes, services with prices, and certificates.
2. **Appear in search.** Farmers search on the Vet Services page by words in your name, tagline, description and specialties, and filter by district. Your profile is active from the first day. Verification by an administrator adds the Verified badge.
3. **When a booking arrives.** It appears under **Appointments** as **Requested** (via marketplace), and in your Overview counts. The app does not send you an email or text message about it, so check the app every day. Confirm it, phone the farmer, and continue as in "receive and manage a farmer's booking".
4. **Keep your rating high.** Reviews from farms appear in the Customer reviews card after moderation.

> **Note:** The page on the farmer's side also has a **Register your practice** button. Anything saved with that button is stored only on that farmer's device and is *not* your real marketplace profile. Use your own workspace, as described above.

> **Watch out:** Check how you appear. Open the farm workspace Vet Services page (or ask a friendly farmer to) after you save your profile, and make sure your practice is in the list. If your practice does not appear, tell AGRINUXES support.

---

## Chapter 3 — Equipment Hire

The Equipment Hire workspace is for businesses that rent out tractors, ploughs, planters, sprayers, drones, trucks and other machines, with or without an operator. Its tabs are:

| Tab | What it does |
|---|---|
| **Overview** | Machines available, open bookings, service due, money received, and bookings needing action. |
| **Fleet Management** | Your list of machines with rates. |
| **Bookings** | Enquiries, quotes and hire jobs from request to payment. |
| **Maintenance** | Service and repair log with due dates and costs. |
| **Clients, Invoices, Revenue, Marketplace Listing, Users & Permissions, Settings** | The common tabs from Chapter 1. |

In **Marketplace Listing** the services card is titled **Other services**, with the hint: "Optional: land preparation packages, delivery, operator-only hire. Machines themselves are listed under Fleet."

### Overview (Equipment Hire)

| Card | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Machines | Available machines out of all machines, for example "3 / 5", with "available now". |
| Open bookings | Bookings in status Enquiry, Quoted, Confirmed or Active; under it, how many are new enquiries. |
| Service due (14 d) | Number of machines with a next-service date within 14 days (or already passed). |
| Received (30 d) | Money received in the last 30 days. |

Quick actions: **Bookings**, **Fleet**, **Invoices**, **Maintenance**. Below, **Bookings needing action** lists up to eight open bookings (reference, client, machine, dates, status, amount). If empty: "No open bookings."

### Fleet Management

**What it is for.** The catalogue of machines you hire out, with the rates you charge. Bookings can be tied to a machine, and the Overview counts how many machines are available.

**Before you start.** Know each machine's name, type and rates.

**Step by step: add a machine**

1. Click **+ Add machine**.
2. In the window **Add to fleet**, fill in:

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Name * | Required. Something you will recognise, for example "John Deere 5075E (Blue)". |
| Type | Tractor, Plough / ridger, Planter, Combine harvester, Sprayer, Agricultural drone, Irrigation system, Truck / transport, Generator, Storage equipment, Processing equipment, Other. A new machine starts as Tractor. |
| Make / Model / Year | Brand, model and year of manufacture. |
| Capacity (HP, tonnes, m) | Free text, for example "75 HP" or "3.5 t". |
| Daily rate (ZMW) | The price per day. |
| Per-hectare / per-km rate (ZMW) | The price per hectare (or per km for vehicles). |
| Minimum hire days | The shortest hire you accept. Starts at 1. |
| Operator / driver included | Tick if the price includes an operator. Starts ticked. |
| Fuel included | Tick if fuel is in the price. |
| Available for hire | Tick when the machine can be booked. Starts ticked. |
| Notes | Anything else, for example conditions or the delivery charge. |

3. Click **Save**.

**What the table shows:** machine name with make, model and year; type; capacity; daily rate; per-hectare rate; "operator / no operator · fuel incl. / fuel excl."; whether it is available (a tick, or "unavailable" in amber); **Edit** and **Delete** buttons.

**Good to know**

- Rates are in ZMW. Fill in whichever of daily and per-hectare rates applies; the booking form uses them to suggest a price (see below).
- To take a machine out of service for repairs, open **Edit** and untick **Available for hire**. Logging maintenance does *not* do this for you.
- You can only delete a machine that has no Confirmed or Active bookings.
- The fleet page says the entries are visible to farmers and can be booked. At present a farmer's booking form asks for dates, hectares, address and notes, not for a specific machine, so you will normally choose the machine yourself when you quote.

**If something goes wrong:** "Equipment has confirmed or active bookings — mark it unavailable instead" means you tried to delete a machine that is booked. Untick **Available for hire** instead.

### Bookings

**What it is for.** To manage each hire from the first enquiry to the final payment, whether it came from a farmer on the marketplace or from a phone call.

#### Booking statuses

| Status shown | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Enquiry | A request arrived, or you logged an enquiry. Nothing is agreed yet. |
| Quoted | You gave the client a price. |
| Confirmed | The client accepted. The machine is reserved for the dates. |
| Active | The machine is out with the client (in use, on site). |
| Completed | The hire is finished and the machine is back. |
| Cancelled | The hire will not happen. |

A normal booking moves **Enquiry → Quoted → Confirmed → Active → Completed**. You can set any status from the **Update** window; the app does not force the order, except that a quote needs an amount.

#### The bookings table

The **Bookings** card has a status filter and a **+ Booking** button. Columns:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Ref | The reference such as HB-2026-0012, and "marketplace" or "walk-in". |
| Client | Name, then phone or district. |
| Equipment | The machine, or "unassigned" in amber. |
| Dates | Start → end. |
| Area | Hectares, if given. |
| Quote / agreed | The agreed amount, or "quote" and the quoted amount. |
| Paid | A tick if paid in full, "deposit" if only the deposit is paid. |
| Status | Status label. |

The last column has **Quote** (on an Enquiry) or **Update** (on anything else), and **Invoice** (when Completed and not paid in full).

#### Step by step: handle a booking from a farmer

1. A farmer sends a hire request. It appears in **Bookings** as **Enquiry**, "marketplace", with the machine shown as **unassigned**, and it counts under **Open bookings** and in the Overview list **Bookings needing action**.
2. Click **Quote**. The window with the booking reference and client name opens. If the farmer wrote a note, you see "Customer note: …" at the top.
3. Choose the **Equipment** (the machine) and check **Start** and **End** dates.
4. Enter the **Quoted amount (ZMW)**. Set **Status** to **Quoted**.
5. In **Notes to customer** write what the price includes, for example "Includes operator, fuel extra, delivery ZMW 300".
6. Click **Save**. The message "[reference] updated." appears. Phone the farmer with the quote; the app does not send it.
7. When the farmer accepts, click **Update**, set **Agreed amount (ZMW)**, and change **Status** to **Confirmed**.
8. When the machine goes out, set **Status** to **Active**. Tick **Deposit paid** if you took a deposit.
9. When it is back, set **Status** to **Completed**. Tick **Paid in full** when the money is received, or use **Invoice**.

**The Update window fields**

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Status | Enquiry, Quoted, Confirmed, Active, Completed, Cancelled. |
| Equipment | The machine, or **Unassigned**. |
| Start / End | The hire dates. End must not be before start. |
| Quoted amount (ZMW) | The price you offered. |
| Agreed amount (ZMW) | The price agreed. This is the amount revenue and invoices use. |
| Deposit paid | A tick box. |
| Paid in full | A tick box. |
| Notes to customer | Your notes. |

> **Watch out:** The deposit is only a tick box. The app does not record how much the deposit was. Write the amount in **Notes to customer** or in the invoice notes.

> **Watch out:** The app does **not** check whether a machine is already booked on the same dates. Before you confirm, look at **Bookings** for the same machine and dates, to avoid double booking.

> **Watch out:** **Paid in full** adds money to **Revenue** only if the booking also has an **Agreed amount**. Fill it in before you tick paid.

#### Step by step: log a phone or walk-in booking

1. Click **+ Booking**.
2. Choose the client with the client picker.
3. Fill in the **New booking** window:

| Field | What to enter |
|---|---|
| Equipment | A machine, or **Choose later**. Machines that are not available show "(unavailable)". |
| Status | Enquiry, Quoted, Confirmed or Active. Starts as Confirmed. |
| Start * / End * | Dates. Both start as today. |
| Hectares | The area to be worked, if hired by area. |
| Agreed amount (ZMW) | The price. If you chose a machine, the label shows a suggestion, for example "suggested 4800". |
| Site / delivery address | Where the machine will work. |
| Notes | Your own note. |

4. Click **Create booking**. "Booking created." appears.
